Finally, we demonstrate transfer to a physical G1 humanoid using whole-body policies trained with reinforcement learning on the retargeted references, across motions including two-handed box pick-and-place onto a table.

</details>


### [36] [RoboChrono: A Real Robot Benchmark for Streaming Task Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36605)
*Yuzhou Wu,Longteng Fan,Zimeng Li,Yu Wanchan,Ting Zhang,Yiyang Ma,Shihao Li,Wei Ying,Jianbin Qin,Jiajian Jing,Fangwen Chen,Yifan Wu,Zichen Zhang,Ruiqi Yang,Weibin Kong,Yihang Xu,Haoran Liu,Zonghang He,Xuyang Liu,YiFan Xiong,Siteng Huang,Tao Xu,Zhuo Xu,Long Chen,Ruoxiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboChrono 基准，用于评估机器人操作中的流式任务理解，包含 39 个场景、34,713 个评测实例，涵盖识别、对齐与时间定位七类任务；对 18 个视觉语言模型的零样本评测显示，各任务间能力差异显著，强视觉匹配并不等于强时间排序，且下一动作预测可依赖任务先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解进行中的机器人操作，要求模型将视觉观察与交互历史、任务进度联系起来，但现有评测多依赖聚合分数，难以刻画不同能力维度的差异，因此需要一个诊断性基准来分能力评测。

Method: 构建 RoboChrono 基准，数据来自真实机器人执行与配套的裸手人类录制，共 39 个场景、34,713 个评测实例；设置识别、对齐、时间定位三类共七项任务，覆盖动作理解与预测、视觉对应、时间排序、动作定位；对 18 个视觉语言模型进行零样本评测，并对 5 个开源权重模型做输入消融实验。

Result: 模型表现任务间差异明显：GPT-6-Astra 在帧匹配上达 98.3%，帧排序仅 68.3%；RynnBrain1.1-122B-A10B 差距更大，分别为 95.4% 与 32.9%。消融显示去视觉观察后当前动作识别下降 22.1 个百分点，而下一动作预测仅降 0.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 强的视觉匹配能力并不必然伴随强的时间排序能力，下一动作预测即使缺少视觉证据也可由任务与动作先验支撑；评估机器人操作任务理解需超越聚合分数，进行能力特定的诊断性评测，RoboChrono 提供了这一设定。

Abstract: Understanding ongoing robot manipulation requires models to interpret visual observations in relation to interaction history and task progress. We introduce RoboChrono, a benchmark for streaming task understanding comprising 39 scenarios and 34,713 evaluation instances, constructed from real robot executions and complementary bare-hand human recordings. The benchmark evaluates seven tasks grouped into recognition, alignment, and temporal grounding, covering action understanding and anticipation, visual correspondence, temporal ordering, and action localization. Zero-shot evaluation of 18 vision-language models reveals substantial differences across tasks. GPT-6-Astra achieves 98.3% accuracy on Frame Matching but 68.3% on Frame Ordering, while RynnBrain1.1-122B-A10B exhibits a larger gap, reaching 95.4% and 32.9%, respectively. Input ablations on matched questions with five open-weight models further reveal distinct dependencies on visual evidence: removing visual observations reduces Current Action Recognition accuracy by 22.1 percentage points, whereas Next Action Prediction decreases by only 0.7 points. These findings show that strong visual matching does not consistently coincide with strong temporal ordering, and suggest that next-action prediction can be supported by task and action priors even when visual evidence is unavailable. RoboChrono provides a diagnostic setting for examining these differences, highlighting the need for capability-specific evaluation beyond aggregate scores when assessing task understanding in robot manipulation.

</details>


### [37] [Foundation-Model-Guided Topology-Aware Semantic Risk Fields for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36640)
*Giung Lee,Weihang Guo,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由基础模型引导、拓扑感知的语义风险场，将成对方向性风险先验与三维几何结合，用于机器人操作的超越避碰的安全规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常环境中的机器人运动规划不仅要满足硬几何约束，还需考虑依赖上下文的语义风险，传统避碰方法无法刻画这类风险。

Method: 针对每个被操作物体与场景物体对，由基础模型给出六个方向性风险权重和成对空间衰减尺度；结合体素化三维几何，采用拓扑感知屏蔽与测地空间衰减；GPU并行后端批量计算物体级距离与风险，构建稠密三维语义风险场，作为下游规划的模块化代价。

Result: 在完整与部分屏障下评估屏蔽行为，并与逐像素语义先验基线比较三维工作空间表示；三个家庭仿真场景中，所提场优化轨迹在相同几何约束下语义暴露低于仅避碰轨迹，并评估了流水线的计算实用性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该语义风险场可作为超越避碰的、实用的拓扑感知语义代价表示，用于操作规划。

Abstract: Robot motion planning in everyday environments must satisfy hard geometric constraints while accounting for context-dependent semantic risk. We present a foundation-model-guided, topology-aware semantic risk field that extends manipulation safety beyond collision avoidance. For each manipulated-object/scene-object pair, a foundation model provides six directional risk weights and a pair-specific spatial decay scale. The method combines these priors with voxelized 3D scene geometry using topology-aware shielding and geodesic spatial decay. A GPU-parallel backend batches object-level distance and risk computations to construct a dense 3D field that serves as a modular cost for downstream motion planning. We evaluate the field's shielding behavior under full and partial barriers and compare its 3D workspace representation with a pixel-wise semantic-prior baseline. Across three household simulation scenarios, trajectories optimized with the proposed field have lower semantic exposure than collision-only trajectories under the same geometric constraints. We also evaluate the computational practicality and reliability of the supporting pipeline. Together, these results support the proposed field as a practical topology-aware semantic cost representation for manipulation planning beyond collision avoidance.

</details>


### [38] [Where Predictive Supervision Goes Shapes What VLA Policies Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36645)
*Hanseul Kim,Jewon Yeom,Youngjoon Jeong,Minsoo Jo,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究未来预测监督对VLA策略视觉表征的作用机制，发现不同预测接口产生的预测与表征差异显著，且越直接、与场景匹配的未来监督越能提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来预测被广泛用于改进视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，其前提是预测场景演化有助于学习对控制有用的表征。但预测质量本身并不能证明策略学到了更好的动作表征，在分布偏移下，成功控制依赖保留空间状态和对陌生场景变化的预测能力，因此需要弄清何种因素决定预测监督是否真正改善VLA策略的视觉表征。

Method: 通过控制变量比较，匹配目标构造、预测时域和训练条件，考察不同预测接口对预测结果与视觉表征的影响，并追踪预测误差如何塑造策略的视觉流。

Result: 不同预测接口产生的预测与视觉表征差异明显，包括可迁移到陌生场景的空间、动态与动作信息；采用更直接、与场景匹配的未来监督训练的VLA策略，在模拟与真实分布偏移下表现出更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 未来预测应被视为表征学习的设计问题，其对控制的价值取决于监督信号能否作用于策略实际使用的表征。

Abstract: Future prediction is increasingly used to improve vision-language-action (VLA) policies, based on the premise that anticipating scene evolution encourages representations useful for control. However, forecast quality alone does not establish that a policy has learned a better representation for action. This distinction matters under distribution shift, where successful control depends on preserving spatial state and likely scene change beyond familiar configurations. We study what determines whether predictive supervision improves the visual representation used by a VLA policy. Through controlled comparisons with matched target constructions, prediction horizons, and training conditions, we find that different prediction interfaces produce markedly different forecasts and visual representations, including in the spatial, dynamics, and action information that transfers beyond familiar scenes. We trace these differences to how predictive errors shape the policy's visual stream. Consistent with this controlled finding, VLA policies trained with more direct, scene-matched future supervision show stronger robustness under simulated and physical distribution shifts. Together, our results frame future prediction as a representation-learning design problem whose value for control depends on whether its supervision reaches the representations through which the policy acts.

</details>


### [39] [Kinematic Nonlinear Spatio-Temporal Trajectory Warping for Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36676)
*Hyojae Park,Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于接触分布的高质量非线性轨迹变换方法，用于复用已有的富接触灵巧操作演示，并支持障碍物、中间路点、时间偏移和不同起止配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有富接触灵巧操作演示数据获取成本高，直接复用困难，需要一种能适配新任务约束（中间路点、障碍物、时间变化、起止配置变化）的轨迹重定向/变换方法。

Method: 以手和物体轨迹为输入，利用接触分布作为核心表示，采用简单的以物体为中心的warp specification流程，计算复杂高维灵巧手轨迹，输出非线性轨迹变换。

Result: 在公开人手运动数据集上，基于4个演示构造12种变化进行评估，并与基线方法比较，同时展示了方法在不同操作器上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法简单有效，能够可靠复用现有富接触灵巧操作演示并生成满足新约束的高质量轨迹，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: We present a straightforward but effective method for repurposing existing contact-rich dexterous manipulation demonstrations. Starting from inputs of hand and object trajectories, our method outputs high-quality nonlinear trajectory warps that account for intermediate waypoints, environmental barriers, temporal shifts, and varied start/end configurations. Foundational to our method is the utilization of contact distributions, which we show allows us to reliably compute complex and high-dimensional dexterous hand trajectories following a simple object-centric warp specification pipeline. We evaluate our method across 12 variations sourced from 4 demonstrations in a publicly available dataset of human hand motion data, perform baseline comparisons, and demonstrate generalization of our approach to different manipulators. Results and code will be made available on publication.

</details>


### [40] [T$^2$Mem: Learning Test-Time Memory for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36720)
*Yize Liu,Huang Huang,Yining Hong,Zijian Du,Zhi Cao,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 T²Mem 框架，让无记忆的预训练视觉-语言-动作模型通过测试时训练将历史观测编码为快速权重，实现记忆依赖的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖的机器人操作需要策略利用当前观测中已缺失的信息，仅保留历史并不够，记忆必须保存支持未来动作的信息。核心挑战在于：无记忆的基础模型能否仅凭动作示范，在没有外部记忆支持的情况下学会保留并使用历史信息。

Method: 无需外部推理模型或记忆专用标注，在预训练 VLA 策略内发展该能力。T²Mem 使用测试时训练，通过在线自监督更新将观测历史编码为紧凑的快速权重，避免重复处理完整历史；采用观测锚定的接口提取视觉-语言信息用于记忆形成，并向动作专家提供检索到的上下文。动作监督塑造记忆学习保留与使用的内容，交替的记忆-策略学习让各组件在优化时拥有固定的对应部分。

Result: 在 16 项 RoboMME 任务上，平均成功率从无记忆基础策略的 17.93% 提升至 56.83%，并优于基准中报告的循环记忆方法；受控剖析显示相比显式方法至少有 3 倍推理加速。

Conclusion: T²Mem 证明无记忆基础模型可仅凭动作示范自主学习记忆能力，在保持高效推理的同时显著提升长时序操作任务成功率。

Abstract: Memory-dependent robotic manipulation requires policies to use information that is no longer available in the current observation. Retaining history alone is insufficient: memory must preserve information that supports future actions. One challenge is whether a memory-free foundation model can learn to retain and use historical information from action demonstrations alone, without external memory support. We introduce T$^2$Mem, a framework that develops this capability within a pretrained vision-language-action policy, without external reasoning models or memory-specific annotations. T$^2$Mem uses test-time training to encode observation history into compact fast weights through online self-supervised updates, avoiding repeated processing of the full history. An observation-grounded interface extracts vision-language information for memory formation and supplies retrieved context to the action expert. Action supervision shapes what the memory learns to retain and use, while alternating memory-policy learning gives each component a fixed counterpart during optimization. Across 16 RoboMME tasks, T$^2$Mem improves average success from 17.93% to 56.83% over the memory-free base policy and outperforms the recurrent-memory methods reported in the benchmark, while controlled profiling indicates at least 3x inference speedup over explicit methods. Project website: https://yzliu84.github.io/T2MEM-project/

</details>


### [41] [Degeneracy-Orthogonal Geometric Constraints for LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36753)
*Minseo Kim,Yina Kim,Jinhwa Hwang,Alex Junho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeCOD跨截面几何描述子，通过截面边界地标匹配为退化方向提供度量约束，抑制长隧道/管道中LiDAR里程计的纵向漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴对称均匀走廊（长隧道、管道）中，LiDAR里程计沿特征稀疏的行驶方向存在结构退化，仅靠局部扫描匹配无法约束纵向漂移；而截面边界（管道接头、结构环）可提供该退化轴上的度量约束，难点在于区分外观近乎相同的个体地标。

Method: 提出DeCOD描述子：将相对估计边界轮廓的有符号法向偏差参数化；匹配时显式消解航向歧义，并通过畸变估计解耦一阶轮廓误差；匹配地标生成几何因子，在位姿图优化中约束截面位置与走廊轴向对齐，同时保持绕公共轴的旋转不受约束。

Result: 在公开基准与实地实验中，DeCOD的地标检索鲁棒性优于标准3D描述子，并能跨不同里程计前端稳定轨迹，在几何退化条件下可靠约束纵向漂移。

Conclusion: 结构对齐的DeCOD描述子可有效缓解轴向均匀走廊的几何退化问题，为长隧道与管道场景的导航提供可靠的纵向约束。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation relies on simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) to estimate motion and maintain an accurate pose within an environment. However, in axially uniform corridors such as long tunnels and pipelines, LiDAR odometry is fundamentally limited by unconstrained drift along the feature-weak travel direction. This structural degeneracy cannot be resolved by local scan matching alone. To address this challenge, we propose the Degeneracy-orthogonal Contour Offset Descriptor (DeCOD), a structure-aligned geometric descriptor for cross-sectional landmarks. Cross-sectional boundaries, such as pipe joints and structural rings, provide metric constraints along this degenerate axis, but distinguishing individual landmarks requires capturing subtle surface variations across nearly identical profiles. The descriptor parameterizes signed normal deviation from estimated boundary contours, and matching explicitly resolves heading ambiguity and decouples first-order contour errors by distortion estimation. Matched landmarks yield geometric factors that enforce agreement in cross-section position and corridor axis alignment during pose-graph optimization, correcting longitudinal drift while leaving rotation about the common axis unconstrained. On a public benchmark and in field experiments, DeCOD achieves robust landmark retrieval over standard 3D descriptors and successfully stabilizes trajectories across different odometry frontends, reliably constraining longitudinal drift under geometric degeneracy.

</details>


### [42] [LexiconVLA: Learning Reusable Atomic Action Codebooks for Unseen Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36774)
*Zeming Wei,Jianheng Ye,Xinshuai Song,Sirui Chen,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LexiconVLA，一种可检索的原子动作词典，用于跨任务复用重复性交互；通过全局与细节码本、视觉-原子动作对齐实现，在多个策略骨干和真实机器人上提升未见任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型难以在未见任务中复用重复出现的交互。诊断研究发现，任务能够可靠完成并不意味着其组成原子动作在不同任务情境下能被一致执行，因此需要一种可跨任务复用的原子动作表示机制。

Method: 提出 LexiconVLA 可检索原子动作词典：用全局码本捕获共享交互结构、细节码本捕获细粒度执行变化；通过视觉-原子动作对齐，将视觉状态变化的轨迹重建与动作码的视觉结果预测结合，使词典同时锚定运动与效果；在包含 69 个任务、57,803 段轨迹的 AtomAction 数据集上以轨迹重建和视觉对齐学习码本；再由规划器与场景感知适配器将新目标转化为码条件子任务，交给共享策略执行，无需技能专家或部署时参数更新。

Result: 在 26 个 RLBench 任务、五种策略骨干上，LexiconVLA 在 18 个已见任务上基本保持性能，并提升 8 个策略训练中未出现任务的成功率；搭配 BridgeVLA 时未见任务成功率从 16.67% 提升至 34.17%（+17.50 个百分点），总体成功率达 71.08%，为已报告结果方法中最高。真实机器人实验展示了分步执行与失败恢复能力。

Conclusion: LexiconVLA 通过可检索原子动作词典实现了跨任务的原子动作复用，在不牺牲已见任务性能的前提下显著提升未见任务泛化，并在真实机器人上验证了分步执行与恢复能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle to reuse recurring interactions in unseen tasks. Our diagnostic study reveals that reliable task completion does not imply consistent execution of constituent atomic actions across task contexts. We present LexiconVLA, a retrievable atomic-action lexicon for cross-task reuse. Global and detail codebooks capture shared interaction structure and fine-grained execution variation, respectively, preserving both reusable patterns and execution details. Visual-Atomic Action Alignment couples trajectory reconstruction from visual state changes with visual outcome prediction from action codes, grounding the lexicon in motion and its effects. We learn these codebooks with trajectory reconstruction and visual alignment on our AtomAction Dataset of 57,803 segments from 69 tasks. A planner and scene-aware adapter translate new goals into code-conditioned subtasks for a shared policy, without skill-specific experts or deployment-time parameter updates. Across five policy backbones on 26 RLBench tasks, LexiconVLA largely maintains performance on 18 seen tasks while improving success on 8 tasks held out from policy training. With BridgeVLA, unseen-task success rises from 16.67% to 34.17% (+17.50 percentage points), and overall success reaches 71.08%, the highest among methods with reported results. Real-robot experiments demonstrate stepwise execution and failure recovery.

</details>


### [43] [DRHeC: Differentiable Rendering for Hand-Eye Calibration with RGB-Based Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36779)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Yusheng Wang,Naoya Kagawa,Noritaka Takamura,Keiji Okuhara,Hiroyasu Baba,Jun Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RGB的可微渲染手眼标定框架，通过融合颜色与掩码几何特征提供更丰富的几何和外观线索，并引入掩码引导的图像到图像翻译方法保证颜色与几何一致性，从而提升标定精度与优化稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的手眼标定对精密操作至关重要。传统方法依赖标记物，其精度受标记精度和可观测性限制。无标记方法（如基于学习的方法）虽可用单张图像计算手眼变换，但基于可微渲染的前沿方法使用二值掩码，会丢失内部轮廓细节，且存在优化不稳定和局部最小值问题。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于RGB的可微渲染框架，融合颜色和掩码几何特征以提供更丰富的几何与外观线索；并提出掩码引导的图像到图像翻译方法，在翻译过程中显式保持颜色与几何一致性。通过仿真和真实世界实验进行验证。

Result: 在UR5e真实世界实验中，抓取成功率达88.9%，插入成功率达57.4%，分别比最先进的可微渲染手眼标定方法EasyHeC高出46.3和48.1个百分点。结果证明该方法具有强准确性与鲁棒性，并明显优于现有可微渲染方法。

Conclusion: 该RGB可微渲染手眼标定框架有效提升了标定精度与优化稳定性，在真实操作任务中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Accurate hand-eye calibration is crucial for precision manipulation. Traditional methods rely on markers, with their precision dependent on marker accuracy and observability. In contrast, markerless methods, such as learning-based approaches, use deep neural networks to directly extract keypoints or features from images, enabling the computation of hand-eye transformation with a single image and without the need for physical markers. Recently, differentiable rendering-based methods for hand-eye calibration have leveraged physical models to render binary masks and compare them with observations, enabling hand-eye calibration without fiducial markers in the calibration stage and providing interpretable optimization. While the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering methods achieve remarkable accuracy, the use of binary masks can result in the loss of internal profile details, reducing precision. Additionally, these methods can also suffer from unstable optimization and local minima.
  In this study, we propose a novel RGB-based differentiable rendering framework that provides richer geometric and appearance cues by incorporating color and mask geometric features, thereby improving calibration accuracy and optimization stability. Additionally, we propose a mask-guided image-to-image translation method to ensure explicit preservation of color and geometric consistency throughout the translation. Our approach is validated through both simulation and real-world experiments, with results demonstrating strong accuracy and robustness and clear improvements over existing differentiable rendering methods. Our method achieves a grasping success rate of 88.9% and insertion success rate of 57.4% on the UR5e real-world experiment, outperforming the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering hand-eye calibration method EasyHeC by 46.3 and 48.1 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [44] [Scale-Invariant Manipulability Shape Tracking Across Heterogeneous Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36784)
*Geunwoo Kwon,Dong-gyu Lee,Kai Li,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出尺度不变的可操作度形状跟踪方法，用单位行列式代表矩阵，在AIRM度量下推导微分与切空间正交表示，集成到约束二次规划中，实现异构机器人间及人到机器人的可操作度形状迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨系统迁移可操作度时，若目标仅为复现椭球的方向和半轴长度比，匹配绝对尺度并不必要。全矩阵跟踪会同时惩罚形状与绝对尺度差异，即使只需形状匹配也会带来不利影响。

Method: 将仅相差正标量的矩阵视为等价，取其单位行列式代表；推导单位行列式形状代表的微分，并在仿射不变黎曼度量（AIRM）下给出切空间跟踪残差的正交坐标表示；将尺度不变目标与位置、末端方向任务结合，构建约束关节速度二次规划。

Result: 在四个异构机器人上评估机器人间与人到机器人迁移。三个跟随者上，所提方法无需尺度调参即达到9.30×10^-5的端点形状距离。Full方法经运动中的机器人特定目标尺度调参后，在KR500与UR20上仍有0.19-0.31的端点轴比误差。人在环伸展并发任务中，所提方法在四个机器人上均产生沿X方向拉长的双力形状（与人类目标一致），端点位置误差为参考臂长的2.4-5.6%，而Full方法跟踪原始人类椭球误差最高达75%。

Conclusion: 尺度不变的可操作度形状跟踪方法能有效实现异构系统间仅需形状匹配的可操作度迁移，无需绝对尺度调参，在多机器人及人到机器人任务中优于全矩阵跟踪。

Abstract: When transferring manipulability across systems with different sizes and kinematic structures, matching absolute ellipsoid scale may be unnecessary when the goal is to reproduce orientation and semi-axis length ratios. Full-matrix tracking, however, penalizes both shape and absolute-scale differences, even when only shape matching is required. We therefore propose a scale-invariant manipulability shape-tracking method that treats matrices differing only by a positive scalar factor as equivalent and uses their unit-determinant representatives. We derive the differential of the unit-determinant shape representative and an orthonormal coordinate representation of the tangent tracking residual under the affine-invariant Riemannian metric (AIRM). The resulting scale-invariant objective is integrated with position and end-effector direction tasks in a constrained joint-velocity quadratic program. Simulations with four heterogeneous robots evaluate robot-to-robot and human-to-robot transfer. On three followers, the proposed method achieves endpoint shape distances of 9.30 x 10^-5 without scale tuning. With robot-specific target scales tuned during motion, the Full method retains endpoint axis-ratio errors of 0.19-0.31 on KR500 and UR20. For human reaching with concurrent tasks, the proposed method yields dual force shapes elongated along X like the human target on all four robots, with endpoint position errors of 2.4-5.6% of reference arm length versus up to 75% for the Full method tracking the original human ellipsoid.

</details>


### [45] [TaRL: Learning General and Physical Rewards from Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36785)
*Po-Yi Wu,Dao-Jan Chang,Shang-Ya Hsiao,Hong-Ming Chen,Yu-Cheng Su,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Tactile Reward Learning (TaRL)，从触觉演示中学习奖励，用于接触密集型操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作要求精确的接触序列、稳定抓取和定向力。强化学习可自动获得此类行为，但依赖奖励设计：稀疏奖励效率低，密集奖励难设定。视觉奖励学习虽可从无动作演示中推断奖励，但仅以视觉观测为条件，无法捕捉视觉目标之外的奖励。

Method: TaRL以触觉变形图序列为输入，从成功与失败的演示中回归任务完成进度。由于捕捉局部机器人与物体交互，可提供有效反馈以学习稳定抓取和正确方向的力，并对物体位置等场景布局变化鲁棒。

Result: 在仿真四个任务和真实世界两个任务上评估。作为塑形奖励，显著提升样本效率和最终成功率：仿真中Nut threading成功率从34%提升至56%，真实世界中cube pickup从37%提升至97%。触觉与视觉奖励结合进一步提升性能。TaRL还能跨物体实例泛化：在box placement上训练后直接部署到can placement，显著改善新任务的策略学习。

Conclusion: 触觉奖励学习能有效补充视觉奖励，提升接触密集型操作的学习效率、成功率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to sequence precise contacts, maintain stable grasps, and apply directed forces. Reinforcement learning (RL) can acquire such behaviors automatically, but its performance hinges on reward design: sparse rewards reduce the learning efficiency, while dense rewards are hard to specify. Visual reward learning addresses this by inferring rewards from action-free demonstrations. Because it conditions only on visual observations, it fails to capture rewards beyond visual goals. We propose Tactile Reward Learning (TaRL), a framework that learns rewards from tactile demonstrations. TaRL takes a sequence of tactile deformation maps as input, and regresses task-completion progress from both successful and failed demonstrations. Because TaRL captures local robot-object interaction, it provides informative feedback to learn firm grasps and correctly directed forces; meanwhile, it is robust to changes in scene layout such as object position. We evaluate TaRL on four manipulation tasks in simulation and two in the real world. Used as a shaping reward, it substantially improves both sample efficiency and final success rate, raising success on Nut threading from 34% to 56% in simulation and on cube pickup from 37% to 97% in the real world. Combining tactile with visual rewards improves performance further. TaRL also generalizes across object instances: trained on box placement and directly deployed to can placement, it significantly improves policy learning on the new task. Project page is available at https://embodiedai-ntu.github.io/tarl.

</details>


### [46] [Spotter: Let the Embodied Model Lead, and the VLM Reflect for It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36808)
*Long Li,Qichao Zhao,Yue Yang,Fan Xu,Zhe Wang,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Chao Qu,Heng Tao Shen,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spotter 框架，反转 VLM 与具身模型的主导关系：具身模型连续执行任务，VLM 并行监控、仅在被轻量筛查器检测到错误时介入反思并纠正，从而让具身模型具备"从失败中修复"的能力，且开销小、更通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身模型无法对自身失败做出反应，即使刚发生的错误能为下一次尝试提供调整线索。作者测试其能否修复已知错误（需生成纠正并判断正误），发现在失败处停止并允许重试时，模型很少能通过自身随机性或错误的语言描述完成修复，best-of-N 选择也无法在失败后挑出成功候选。作者将其归因于仅用成功示范训练、输入信息过窄，故认为反思必须来自能接收更多信息（如回合历史与文本）且更通用的视觉语言模型（VLM）。

Method: 提出 Spotter，反转以往 VLM 主导的角色：由具身模型主导并连续执行，VLM 并行运行，通过轻量级本地筛查器监控，仅在检测到错误时介入，进行反思与纠正，随后交还控制权。用 Qwen 和 GPT 分别作为 VLM 进行实验。

Result: 两种 VLM 均能提升具身模型表现。使用 GPT 时，Spotter 在 RoboCasa 上将 Cosmos Policy 与 π0.5 分别提升 5.6 和 7.5 个百分点；将 π0.5 在 RoboTwin 2.0 的 Hard 设置上从 47.2% 提升至 57.0%，在真实机器人上从 53% 提升至 83%。由于 VLM 仅在确认错误时介入，使用 Qwen 时成功回合仅比单用具身模型多花 13 至 16 秒，比使用同一模型的 VLM 主导基线少约 70% 时间。

Conclusion: 反思能力须来自信息更丰富、更通用的 VLM；通过让具身模型主导执行、VLM 并行监控并在错误确认时介入的 Spotter 框架，可在较低时间开销下显著提升具身模型的失败修复与任务成功率。代码已开源。

Abstract: Current embodied models do not respond to their own failures, although what just went wrong could inform a small adjustment on the next attempt, the kind of reflection behind the gains of thinking in language models. We test whether they can repair a known error, which requires producing a correction and judging whether it is right. Stopped at a failure and allowed to retry, they seldom repair it through their own randomness or from a language description of the error, and best-of-N selection cannot pick the successful candidate after a failure. We attribute this to training only on successful demonstrations and to inputs too narrow to show what went wrong, and conclude that reflection must come from a vision-language model (VLM), which takes in far more information, such as the episode history and text, and is more general. Prior VLM-led work has the VLM plan every step and invoke the embodied model as a tool, placing the VLM on the critical path. We propose Spotter, which reverses the roles: the embodied model leads and executes continuously, while the VLM runs in parallel, monitors through a lightweight local screener, intervenes only when an error is detected, reflects on and corrects it, and returns control. We run Spotter with Qwen and with GPT as the VLM, and both improve the embodied models; with GPT, Spotter improves Cosmos Policy and $π_{0.5}$ by 5.6 and 7.5 percentage points on RoboCasa, and raises $π_{0.5}$ from 47.2% to 57.0% on the Hard setting of RoboTwin 2.0 and from 53% to 83% on a real robot. Because the VLM steps in only when an error is confirmed, a successful episode with Qwen takes only 13 to 16 s longer than with the embodied model alone and about 70% less time than with a VLM-led baseline using the same model. Our code is available at https://github.com/zqc3117/Spotter.

</details>


### [47] [VidAct: Learning Manipulation from In-the-Wild Videos with Object-Centric 3D Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36870)
*Hang Li,Mingxin Zhang,Zihan Wu,Yang Tian,Dong Chen,Fengyi Shen,Yuan Meng,Xiangtong Yao,Heng Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Zhenshan Bing,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VidAct：从单目视频学习物体中心、3D感知的操作策略，实现零样本真机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重建的视频学习方法依赖受限视角或人-机重定向，轨迹难以适应新物体配置且易变形；策略缺乏精确的物体级3D几何感知，限制物体定位与形状感知。作者希望提出一种高效视频到机器人框架，从单个单目视频学习物体中心、3D感知的操作策略并支持零样本真实部署。

Method: VidAct包含三部分：(1) 从任意演示视频重建物体网格与运动，并在静态物体坐标系中做规范化，避免具身特定重定向并适应多样视角；(2) 采用残差轨迹迁移，将重建运动适配到新物体配置同时保持运动形状；(3) 策略学习关键组件：预测仿真提供的特权完整物体点云作为辅助任务，部署时仅用RGB，从而提供对物体位姿和3D几何的密集物体中心监督。

Result: 在人类、机器人、生成及互联网视频上实验，展示广泛的视频适用性与零样本部署；逐帧完整物体3D监督提升策略泛化与sim-to-real成功率；残差轨迹迁移实现可靠轨迹适配并更好保持形状。

Conclusion: VidAct通过物体中心3D感知策略学习与残差轨迹迁移，有效利用单目视频实现零样本真机操作，验证了完整物体3D监督与轨迹迁移的重要作用。

Abstract: Video demonstrations offer a scalable alternative to costly robot data for learning manipulation, yet existing reconstruction-based approaches often rely on constrained camera viewpoints or human-to-robot retargeting, while the reconstructed trajectories are difficult to adapt to new objects configurations without distorting the trajectory shape. Another key limitation is that the resulting policies often lack precise object-level 3D geometry awareness, limiting object grounding and object shape awareness critical for precise manipulation. To bridge these gaps, we propose VidAct, an efficient video-to-robot framework that learns object-centric, 3D-aware manipulation policies from a single monocular video per task and enables zero-shot real-world deployment. VidAct consists of three key components. First, VidAct reconstructs object meshes and motion from arbitrary demo videos and canonicalizes the motion in the static object frame, avoiding embodiment-specific retargeting and accommodating diverse camera viewpoints. Second, VidAct employ residual trajectory transfer for adapting the reconstructed motion to novel object configurations while preserving its motion shape. Finally, as the key policy-learning component, VidAct predicts simulation-provided privileged complete-object point clouds at each frame as an auxiliary task while retaining RGB-only deployment, providing dense object-centric supervision over both object pose and 3D geometry. Experiments on human, robot, generated, and internet videos demonstrate broad video applicability and zero-shot deployment. Per-frame complete-object 3D supervision improves policy generalization and sim-to-real success, while residual trajectory transfer enables reliable trajectory adaptation with better shape preservation.

</details>


### [48] [PreferenceFlow: Test-Time Guidance of Flow-Matching Robot Policies from Human Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36872)
*Yiqi Tang,Diyuan Shi,Runze Li,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PreferenceFlow是一个在测试时利用人类干预作为局部偏好监督、无需环境奖励或更新基座策略即可改进预训练流匹配策略的框架，在四个真实精密插入任务上平均成功率达90.5%（冻结策略为69%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略能表示复杂机器人行为，但在部署时的分布偏移下易受局部错误影响。许多强化学习的策略改进方法需要难以指定或获取的奖励信号，这在真实世界操作中尤为困难。因此需要一种无需环境奖励、无需更新基座策略的策略改进方法。

Method: 将人类干预片段与从相同初始条件状态生成的机器人片段配对以训练偏好模型。推理时采用QGF采样更新，用估计干净动作处评估的偏好梯度替换其价值梯度。引入梯度上限损失惩罚干预对上的过大梯度，并用零梯度损失抑制专家演示动作附近的引导。

Result: 在Franka机器人的四个真实世界精密插入任务上，PreferenceFlow平均成功率达90.5%，而冻结策略为69%。消融实验支持梯度正则化和正确排序偏好标签在所评估设置中的作用。

Conclusion: 结果证明了人类干预作为局部偏好监督在引导冻结生成式机器人策略方面的实用性。

Abstract: Flow-matching policies can represent complex robot behaviors but remain susceptible to local errors under distribution shift at deployment. Many reinforcement learning approaches to policy improvement require reward signals that are difficult to specify or obtain in real-world manipulation. We present PreferenceFlow, a framework for improving a pretrained flow policy at test time without environment rewards or updates to the base policy. Human intervention chunks are paired with robot chunks generated from the same initial conditioning state to train a preference model. During infer- ence, we adopt the QGF sampling update, replacing its value gradient with the preference gradient evaluated at an estimated clean action. A gradient-cap loss penalizes excessive gradients on intervention pairs, while a zero-gradient loss discourages guidance near actions from expert demonstrations. On four real-world precision insertion tasks with a Franka robot, Pref- erenceFlow achieves a mean success rate of 90.5%, compared with 69% for the frozen policy. Ablations support the roles of gradient regularization and correctly ordered preference labels in the evaluated settings. These results demonstrate the utility of human interventions as local preference supervision for guiding frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [49] [NIDAR: NIR-Guided Intrinsic Decomposition for Scalable Scene-Agnostic LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36878)
*Junjie Zhang,Jie Yin,Kefei Qian,Jie Li,Mengpei Jia,Yajuan Dun,Wenbo Chu,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NIDAR 是一个前馈框架，从 RGB 外观与仿真器几何中合成稠密类强度观测，无需目标场景强度标签或逐场景梯度拟合，可迁移到仿真点云并集成到多种仿真与 SLAM 管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 回波强度为机器人感知与状态估计提供互补的表面响应线索，但许多仿真管线省略该信息，或使用需要真实强度监督和逐场景优化的重建方法，导致数据采集与拟合成本高、难以跨仿真场景复用。

Method: NIDAR 结合预训练伪近红外（pseudo-NIR）转换、分层本征分解、几何感知调制与源域分布校准，将反射率相关的图像线索迁移到仿真点云；学习组件用 Waymo 数据离线训练，权重与校准在 Waymo 和 nuScenes 评估时保持固定。

Result: 对比显示逐像素精度具有竞争力，结构保真度与感知保真度优于所评估的重建基线；受控的伪近红外与 RGB 诊断表明，伪近红外先验经论文所述反射率与重映射路径使用时收益最大，而非直接作为强度回归器。与 Unreal Engine 5、Isaac Sim 及生成式 LiDAR 管线集成后，两个强度感知 SLAM 系统在两个仿真室内场景中显示出潜在下游效用。

Conclusion: NIDAR 为所评估设置提供了可扩展的强度合成接口，但仿真 SLAM 结果不构成真实机器人验证；跨波长、相机配置、嵌入式部署与真实传感器验证仍属未来工作。

Abstract: LiDAR return intensity provides complementary surface-response cues for robotic perception and state estimation, yet many simulation pipelines omit it or reproduce it using reconstruction methods that require real intensity supervision and per-scene optimization. These requirements increase data-collection and fitting costs and limit reuse across simulated scenes. We present NIDAR, a feed-forward framework that synthesizes dense intensity-like observations from RGB appearance and simulator geometry. NIDAR combines pretrained pseudo-NIR translation, hierarchical intrinsic decomposition, geometry-aware modulation, and source-domain distribution calibration to transfer reflectance-related image cues to simulated point clouds. Its learned components are trained offline using Waymo data; their weights and calibration remain fixed during evaluation on Waymo and nuScenes. Deployment therefore requires neither target-scene intensity labels nor target-scene gradient-based fitting. The reported comparisons show competitive pixel-wise accuracy and favorable structural and perceptual fidelity against the evaluated reconstruction baselines. A controlled pseudo-NIR-versus-RGB diagnostic further shows that the pseudo-NIR prior is most beneficial when used through the paper-aligned reflectance-and-remapping route, rather than as a simple direct intensity regressor. We further integrate NIDAR with Unreal Engine 5, Isaac Sim, and a generative LiDAR pipeline. Two intensity-aware SLAM systems evaluated in two simulated indoor scenes suggest potential downstream utility, but do not constitute real-robot validation. NIDAR therefore offers a scalable intensity-synthesis interface for the evaluated settings; cross-wavelength, camera-configuration, embedded, and real-sensor validation remain future work.

</details>


### [50] [All Roads Lead to Rome: Flow-driven Multi-Anchor Exploration for Open-Environment Active 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36889)
*Yang Li,Aming Wu,Zihao Zhang,Ziju Han,Sijia Zhang,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将开放环境主动3D建图中的长时程目标预测从单点确定性预测重构为基于条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）的条件多模态锚点生成，并结合障碍感知规划、探索模式聚类与分层选择，提升开放环境下的泛化性与重建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动建图方法多基于闭集假设（测试环境与训练环境相似），在仅掌握有限未知环境信息时泛化能力不足。长时程探索通常先预测一个粗略的远距离目标再转化为可执行路径，但该阶段被建模为单点预测；在部分可观测条件下，同一局部观测可能对应多个合理的探索方向，确定性预测容易产生脆弱决策，导致在未见场景中性能下降。实验进一步验证这是其泛化能力弱的关键原因。

Method: 将长时程目标预测重构为使用条件流匹配的条件多模态锚点生成：不再预测单一目标，而是从当前建图状态学习粗略探索锚点的条件分布；锚点经障碍感知规划转换为可执行的候选路径；再通过探索模式聚类压缩几何相似的轨迹、减少候选冗余；最后由分层选择模块选出最有前景的模式，并在该模式内对路径重排序，生成最终可执行轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在开放环境中提升了泛化能力与重建效率。

Conclusion: 通过条件多模态锚点生成替代单点预测，并配合聚类与分层选择，可有效缓解部分可观测下确定性预测的脆弱性，改善开放环境主动3D建图的泛化性与探索重建效率。

Abstract: To advance the development of embodied intelligence, Open-Environment Active 3D Mapping has attracted increasing attention, aiming to perform a long-horizon and shortest trajectory exploration for reconstructing unseen scenarios. Since only limited information about unseen environments is available, methods built on the closed-set assumption, i.e., assuming that the test environments are similar to those seen during training, cannot generalize satisfactorily. In existing active mapping methods, long-horizon exploration is often guided by predicting a coarse long-range goal and then converting it into an executable path. However, this stage is usually formulated as single-point prediction. Under partial observability, the same local observation may correspond to multiple plausible exploration directions, making such deterministic prediction prone to brittle decisions and degraded performance in unseen scenarios. Our experiments further verify that this is a key factor underlying their weak generalization. To address this issue, we reformulate long-horizon target prediction as conditional multimodal anchor generation using Conditional Flow Matching.Instead of predicting a single goal, our method learns a conditional distribution over coarse exploration anchors from the current mapping state. These anchors are first converted into executable candidate paths through obstacle-aware planning. We then apply exploration-mode clustering to compress geometrically similar trajectories and reduce candidate redundancy. Finally, a hierarchical selection module selects the most promising mode and reranks paths within it to produce the final executable trajectory. Experiments show that our method improves generalization and reconstruction efficiency in open environments.

</details>


### [51] [AeroManip-VLA: Scalable Vision-Language-Action Learning for Aerial Manipulation with RL-Generated Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36915)
*Rui Huang,Yanlin Mu,Lidong Li,Yucong Wang,Zichen Yan,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AeroManip-VLA，一个面向空中机械臂视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的可扩展基准，用于数据生成与策略评估，支持并行仿真、自动演示生成、事件标注与基线评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂将操作能力扩展到地面机器人难以到达的三维空间，但将 VLA 模型迁移到空中机器人面临操作与飞行紧耦合、观测持续变化、物理交互安全关键等挑战。这些挑战需要多样化训练数据和系统化策略评估，而直接在真实空中平台上采集演示与评估策略成本高、难以扩展、难以在受控条件下重复。

Method: 构建 GPU 加速的仿真框架，具备大规模并行环境中的底层载荷感知飞行与操作控制；结合可复用的强化学习策略与专家任务规则，在多样物体、环境和随机初始条件下自动生成演示，无需人工遥操作；引入自动事件标注与轨迹分类以筛选演示，支持对任务进度、行为结果和安全相关失败的细粒度分析；并对多种模仿学习与 VLA 基线在不同任务设置下进行评测。

Result: 生成的数据涵盖抓取、放置等基础技能，以及需要导航与操作协同的长时序任务；自动事件标注与轨迹分类机制实现了对任务进度、行为结果和安全失败的细粒度分析；对多种模仿学习与 VLA 基线的评测揭示了其性能特征与失败模式。

Conclusion: AeroManip-VLA 支持可扩展的空中操作数据生成、结构化轨迹分析和系统化 VLA 评估，可在真实部署前于仿真中完成验证。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators extend robotic manipulation into 3D workspaces that are difficult for ground-based robots to access, creating new opportunities for general-purpose manipulation. However, extending Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models to aerial robots introduces distinct challenges due to the tight coupling between manipulation and flight, continuously changing observations, and safety-critical physical interactions. These challenges demand diverse training data and systematic policy evaluation, yet collecting demonstrations and evaluating policies directly on physical aerial platforms are costly, difficult to scale, and hard to repeat under controlled conditions. We present AeroManip-VLA, a scalable benchmark for aerial VLA data generation and policy evaluation. AeroManip-VLA provides a GPU-accelerated simulation framework with low-level payload-aware flight and manipulation control in massively parallel environments. Building on this framework, we combine reusable reinforcement learning policies with expert task rules to automatically generate demonstrations without human teleoperation across diverse objects, environments, and randomized initial conditions. The generated data include basic skills such as grasping and placing, as well as long-horizon tasks that require both navigation and manipulation. We further introduce automated event labeling and trajectory categorization to filter demonstrations. These mechanisms enable fine-grained analysis of task progress, behavioral outcomes, and safety-related failures. Finally, we evaluate a range of imitation learning and VLA baselines across different task settings, revealing their performance characteristics and failure modes. Together, AeroManip-VLA enables scalable aerial manipulation data generation, structured trajectory analysis, and systematic VLA evaluation in simulation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [52] [Track-and-Complete: Learning Humanoid Skills from a Single Failed Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36924)
*Sarmad Idrees,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRACC管线，从失败的演示视频中模仿有效运动前缀，并借助推断的任务结果完成学习，无需成功演示即可训练人形机器人技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人从视频学习技能通常需要成功的人类演示，且往往需要定制化数据采集。失败视频以往仅被当作负样本，但其实蕴含失败前可用的轨迹前缀以及任务预期结果，这些信息可被利用。

Method: TRACC首先模仿失败视频中失败发生前的有效运动前缀，将其作为先验知识；失败发生后，利用推断出的任务结果，通过任务完成奖励引导策略学习预期目标，无需成功轨迹。

Result: 在Oops!数据集的六个真实场景失败人类任务上进行评估，实验结果表明该方法在无成功演示时仍能有效从失败尝试中学习。

Conclusion: 失败的人类视频可作为人形技能学习的可行监督来源，为机器人学习提供了新的数据利用思路。

Abstract: Learning humanoid skills from videos typically requires a successful human demonstration, which often demands custom data collection. Although failures have traditionally been treated only as negative examples in robot learning, they can still reveal a usable trajectory prefix before the task fails, as well as the intended outcome. To leverage this information from a failed-attempt video, we propose TRACC, a pipeline that imitates the useful portion of the motion trajectory and then completes the task based on the inferred task outcome. The usable motion prefix serves as prior knowledge until the failure occurs, after which the task-completion reward guides the policy to learn the intended task goal without requiring a successful task trajectory. We evaluate our method on six in-the-wild failed human tasks from the Oops! dataset. Our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for learning from failed attempts when no successful demonstration is available. Thus, these findings establish failed human videos as a viable source of supervision for humanoid skill learning.

</details>


### [53] [ComManip: Overfitting Manipulation Policies to Comfortable Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36928)
*Yidan Lai,Xinyi Chen,Shuquan Man,Huiping Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ComManip 学习范式，主张将操作策略专门化到"舒适操作区域"（视觉与运动学稳定的紧凑区域），推理时通过移动机器人底座使目标中心进入熟悉图像范围后再执行策略，从而在有限示教预算下大幅提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练机器人操作策略依赖昂贵的示教数据，大规模采集不现实。现有方法为提升泛化性，在数据采集时通过变化物体摆放、视角和机器人构型来增加视觉观测多样性；但在有限示教预算下，这迫使策略建模多样视觉观测，对相似局部条件下的观测-动作对应关系监督不足。作者发现这种策略的任务成功率反而低于在紧凑、视觉与运动学稳定区域内训练的策略，并将此稳定区域称为"舒适操作区域"。

Method: 提出 ComManip 学习范式：将操作策略专门化到舒适操作区域。推理阶段，先重新定位移动基座，直到检测到的目标中心进入由舒适区域示教数据估计出的熟悉图像空间范围，随后执行同一操作策略，从而在不同目标位置实现有效操作。

Result: 在多种操作任务、示教预算和策略族（ACT、π_{0.5}、RDT、OpenVLA-OFT、SmolVLA）上开展大量实验。结果表明，在有限示教预算的大工作空间内，ComManip 在不同策略架构上约提升任务成功率 20 个百分点或更多。

Conclusion: 将操作策略专门化到舒适区域是一种更具数据效率的操作学习范式，可在有限示教条件下有效提升跨目标位置的泛化与成功率。

Abstract: Training robot manipulation policies relies on costly robot demonstrations, making large-scale data collection impractical. Meanwhile, to improve policy generalization, existing approaches seek greater diversity in visual observations by varying object placements, viewpoints, and robot configurations during data collection. However, under a limited demonstration budget, this strategy forces the policy to model diverse visual observations, providing insufficient supervision to learn reliable observation-action correspondences under similar local conditions. Our study reveals that policies trained under this strategy achieve lower task success rates than those trained within a compact, visually and kinematically stable region. We refer to these stable regions as comfortable manipulation regions. To exploit this finding, we propose ComManip, a learning paradigm that specializes manipulation policies to comfortable manipulation regions. During inference, ComManip repositions the mobile base until the detected target center enters the familiar image-space range estimated from comfortable-region demonstrations. It then executes the same manipulation policy, enabling effective manipulation across diverse target locations. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple manipulation tasks, demonstration budgets, and policy families including ACT, $π_{0.5}$, RDT, OpenVLA-OFT, and SmolVLA. The results demonstrate that ComManip improves task success by roughly 20 percentage points or more across different policy architectures in large workspaces under limited demonstration budgets, suggesting that specializing manipulation policies to comfortable regions provides a more data-efficient learning paradigm for manipulation.

</details>


### [54] [Beyond Token Importance: Preserving Spatial Scaffolds for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36967)
*Jiayu Chen,Shuyong Gao,Jingkai Jia,Xiaosheng Bu,Jiyuan Fu,Lingyi Hong,Kaixun Jiang,Yipan Xu,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 现有VLA剪枝按任务语义选择视觉token，忽略操作所需空间信息；作者提出Stride基线并用"空间覆盖半径"诊断问题，据此设计免训练方法GeoScaffold，仅保留20% token仍达93.2%成功率并获1.78倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA视觉token剪枝策略主要依据任务级语义相关性选择单个token，忽视了机器人操作所需的空间信息，导致剪枝后场景空间结构被破坏、任务成功率下降。

Method: 构建按一维展平序列均匀采样的Stride几何剪枝基线；用"空间覆盖半径"（剪枝后保留token集造成的最大空间盲区）刻画现象。提出GeoScaffold：免训练，将图像划分为空间区域，按任务相关性权重分配区域间token预算，并在区域内用最远点采样挑选脚手架token以降低局部覆盖半径。

Result: Stride在部分剪枝比例下优于语义剪枝与随机剪枝，但预算略降即崩溃；空间覆盖半径与任务成功强相关。在pi 0.5与LIBERO上，GeoScaffold仅保留20%视觉token，平均成功率达93.2%，prefill速度较未剪枝基线提升1.78倍。

Conclusion: 可靠的VLA剪枝不仅要保留任务相关token，还需保留场景的空间脚手架；GeoScaffold通过区域预算分配与最远点采样实现高效且高成功率的免训练视觉token剪枝。

Abstract: Existing VLA pruning strategies primarily select individual visual tokens according to task-level semantic relevance, while overlooking the spatial information required for robotic manipulation. To examine this limitation, we construct a simple Stride baseline that uniformly samples tokens along the flattened one-dimensional visual sequence, representing a purely geometric pruning strategy. Surprisingly, Stride outperforms semantic pruning and random pruning at certain pruning ratios, but collapses when the token budget is only slightly reduced. We characterize this phenomenon through the spatial coverage radius, defined as the largest spatial blind spot induced by the retained token set after pruning. Our analysis reveals a strong correlation between the spatial structure of retained tokens and task success, suggesting that reliable VLA pruning requires preserving not only task-relevant tokens but also the spatial scaffold of the scene. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose GeoScaffold, a training-free visual token pruning method that partitions each image into spatial regions, allocates inter-region token budgets using task-relevance weights, and selects intra-region scaffold tokens via farthest point sampling to reduce the local coverage radius. On pi 0.5 and LIBERO, GeoScaffold retains only 20% of visual tokens while preserving a 93.2% average success rate, and achieves a 1.78 times prefill speedup over the unpruned baseline.

</details>


### [55] [All You Need Is Low Fidelity: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real of Learned Robotic Fish Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36993)
*Liam Maloney,Simon Ramchandani,Mike Y. Michelis,Ronan Hinchet,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明对软体欠驱动机器鱼而言，一个刻意低保真、无尾流与附加质量记忆的无状态准稳态流体模型即可训练出可零调参迁移到硬件的通用闭环控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人的复杂任务受限于控制器能力，而为软体欠驱动系统学习控制器时面临"仿真成本与保真度"的权衡。作者希望验证：能否用低成本的低保真仿真器完成可以迁移到真实硬件的控制器学习。

Method: 平台为软体、单电机、腱驱动的机器鱼，策略只观测硬件可测量量。采用分阶段流程，通过两项独立辨识将仿真器锚定：固定尾部动力学与无状态流体模型；策略不直接指令尾部，而是经由带限节律轨迹生成器输出动作。

Result: 在户外水池中未做任何改动部署，单一策略即可完成闭环目标到达、扰动抑制以及分布外目标的获取与跟踪。迁移成功的关键在于"约束"而非保真度：生成器无法超出流体被辨识的频率带。

Conclusion: 借助带限轨迹生成器这一约束，可用低保真仿真器学得可迁移的通用闭环控制器，引发对"物理有多少可放在控制器而非仿真器中"的思考。

Abstract: Complex tasks for underwater robots remain limited by the capabilities of their controllers. Learning a better one for a soft, underactuated robotic fish trades simulator cost against fidelity. We show that an intentionally low-fidelity simulator is enough: a stateless, quasi-steady fluid model with no wake and no added-mass history suffices to learn a \emph{general}, closed-loop controller that transfers to hardware without tuning. Our platform is a soft, single-motor, tendon-driven fish whose policy observes only what the hardware can measure. A staged pipeline grounds the simulator in two independent identifications, fixing the tail dynamics and a stateless fluid model; the policy then acts through a band-limited rhythmic trajectory generator rather than commanding the tail directly. Deployed unchanged in an outdoor pool, a single policy performs closed-loop target reaching, disturbance rejection, and out-of-distribution target acquisition and tracking. The transfer rests on the constraint rather than the fidelity: the generator cannot leave the band over which the fluid was identified. This raises the question of how much of the physics can reside in the controller rather than in the simulator.

</details>


### [56] [FACT: Fidelity-Aware Construction of Articulated Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37067)
*Kuixiang Shao,Chuansen Nie,Yinuo Bai,Jiayuan Gu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FACT智能体框架，逐步构建铰接数字孪生，提升几何、接触与动态保真度，在重建、交互和物理响应上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上合理的铰接资产在接触交互中仍可能失效或出现运动不准确，需提升多维度保真度。

Method: 智能体在可编辑共享表示上执行"证据—诊断—修正"循环；用定量反馈选择测量与模型编辑，数值工具执行并验证；从图像重建可编辑铰接几何，经特征规划、针对性测量与诊断精修；通过任务感知局部重划分修复碰撞代理，再进行保真约束压缩；从被动响应视频构建响应模型，用仿真残差引导修正并进行约束物理参数拟合。

Result: 实验表明FACT优于基线几何重建，使用更简单碰撞代理实现更可靠交互，比直接参数推断更好地复现留出物理响应。

Conclusion: FACT通过智能体闭环构建流程，有效提升铰接孪生的几何、接触与动态保真度。

Abstract: Visually plausible articulated assets may still fail during contact interactions or exhibit inaccurate motion. We present FACT (Fidelity-Aware Construction of Articulated Twins), an agentic framework that progressively constructs articulated twins to improve geometry, contact, and dynamic fidelity. The agent drives an evidence--diagnosis--revision loop on a shared editable representation, selecting measurements and model edits using quantitative feedback, while numerical tools execute and validate the updates. It reconstructs editable articulated geometry from images through feature planning, targeted measurements, and diagnostic refinement. On this reference, it repairs collision proxies through task-aware local repartitioning before fidelity-constrained compression. Finally, it constructs response models from passive-response videos, using simulation residuals to guide model revision and constrained physical parameter fitting. Experiments show that FACT improves geometric reconstruction over baselines, enables more reliable interaction with simpler collision proxies, and better reproduces held-out physical responses than direct parameter inference.

</details>


### [57] [Predictive Safety Curricula for Robust Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37070)
*Ivan Ovinnikov,Pascal Sutter,Christian Gehring,Jordis Herrmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测安全课程（PSC），通过分布化安全评论家预测未来安全成本，优先分配地形与随机事件训练经验，显著提升足式机器人运动可靠性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人运动策略即便平均任务表现很高，仍会残留罕见但后果严重的失败。原因之一在于标准课程主要调整任务难度，而非调整安全关键经验的分布，导致稀有的失败模式未被充分训练。

Method: PSC 从策略 rollout 中训练一个分布化安全评论家，用其对未来安全成本的预测来优先安排地形上下文与先前遇到的随机事件，从而改变训练分布；同时保持任务奖励与策略优化损失不变。评估涵盖受控的崎岖地形运动与真实生产运动系统，并与标准地形递进、优势回放和学习进度课程对比。

Result: PSC 相比标准地形递进、优势回放和学习进度课程提升了可靠性，在困难地形与观测退化时增益最大。在 ANYmal-D 硬件上，跨三个匹配训练种子，PSC 将小腿碰撞发生率相对学习进度课程降低 63%，且每个种子均有下降。在生产爬楼梯平台上，PSC 在评估的硬件试验中消除了观察到的小腿碰撞。

Conclusion: 学习到的未来安全成本预测可为训练经验的分配提供有效信号，将训练导向罕见失败模式，从而提升运动可靠性，并可迁移至多种生产运动系统。

Abstract: Rare but consequential failures can persist in learned locomotion policies for legged robots even when average task performance is high, in part because standard curricula primarily adapt task difficulty rather than the distribution of safety-critical experience. We introduce Predictive Safety Curricula (PSC), a framework for allocating locomotion training experience using learned predictions of future safety cost. PSC trains a distributional safety critic from policy rollouts and uses its predictions to prioritize both terrain contexts and previously encountered randomized events. The resulting curriculum modifies the training distribution while leaving the task reward and policy-optimization loss unchanged. We evaluate PSC in controlled rough-terrain locomotion and in production locomotion systems. PSC improves reliability relative to standard terrain progression, advantage-based replay, and learning-progress curricula, with the largest gains on difficult terrain and under degraded observations. The same allocation principle transfers to two production locomotion stacks. On ANYmal-D hardware, PSC reduces shank-collision incidence by $63\%$ relative to the learning-progress curriculum across three matched training seeds, with a reduction in every seed. On a production stair-climbing platform, PSC eliminates observed shank collisions in the evaluated hardware trials. These results show that learned predictions of future safety cost can provide an effective signal for allocating training experience toward rare failure modes and improving locomotion reliability.

</details>


### [58] [From Sky to Soil: A Morphing Aerial-Ground Robot for Seed Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37072)
*Namai Chandra,Lining Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何辅助、三功能变形机器人，集空中飞行、地面移动和控深播种于一体，通过机构复用实现"飞行-驱动-播种"一体化架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中播种可到达偏远修复地点，但对种子在土壤中的定位控制有限，难以实现精确的控深播种。

Method: 采用几何辅助的三功能变形机器人，着陆后重构为四轮播种构型：电子耦合双电机驱动使后臂外展形成地面车轮，前部托盘下降使推进电机与钻头齿轮组啮合，从而复用推进电机进行钻孔，无需专用钻头驱动器。播种流程为成孔、投放种子、重新定位或恢复飞行构型。配套控制器负责重构、托盘和种子闸门指令，专用自动驾驶仪负责飞行控制。

Result: 通过硬件样机验证了变形与播种机构，演示了地面重新定位和种子埋入功能。

Conclusion: 该概念验证为通过协调重构与执行器复用实现空中-地面种子埋入奠定了硬件基础。

Abstract: Aerial seed broadcasting can reach remote restoration sites, but provides limited control over seed placement within the soil. This paper presents a geometry-assisted, tri-functional morphing robot that combines aerial access, ground locomotion, and controlled-depth seed embedding in a fly-drive-plant architecture. After landing, the platform reconfigures into a four-wheeled planting configuration: an electronically coupled dual-motor drive folds the rear arms outward to form ground wheels, while a descending front tray engages the propulsion motors with a drill gear train. Reusing the propulsion motors for drilling eliminates a dedicated drill drive. The planting sequence forms a hole, dispenses a seed, and allows the vehicle to reposition on the ground or return to its flight configuration. Ground mobility supports repeated planting without requiring a separate flight between adjacent sites. A companion controller issues reconfiguration, tray, and seed-gate commands, while a dedicated autopilot handles flight control. The morphing and planting mechanisms are validated using a hardware prototype that demonstrates ground repositioning and seed embedding. This proof of concept establishes a hardware basis for aerial-ground seed embedding through coordinated reconfiguration and actuator reuse.

</details>


### [59] [BCNav: Bearing-Conditioned Depth Policies for Sound Source Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37084)
*Yaozhong Kang,Jiang Wang,Takeshi Ashizawa,Benjamin Yen,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BCNav：一种解耦框架，将声学感知（DOA估计）与导航策略分离，仅用深度图与方位角作为策略输入，通过模仿学习输出连续速度指令，实现无需真实音频数据的机器人声源导航，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声源导航方法依赖声学模拟器做联合视听学习，但模拟器精度低、成本高，造成仿真到现实的域差距；同时基于网格模拟器的离散动作空间给实体机器人带来运动学差距，导致真实部署不可靠。

Method: 提出BCNav解耦框架：用到达方向（DOA）估计器将声学模块与导航策略分离，估计器为策略提供声源的标量方位角；导航策略只处理深度图像与方位角两类域差距已知的输入；通过注入校准方位噪声的噪声采集最短路径演示，用模仿学习训练策略，直接输出可在地面机器人上执行的连续速度指令。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上完成验证，能够在未知环境中导航至声源，且无需声学微调、无需事先建图，也不需要采集真实世界音频数据。

Conclusion: 通过解耦声学估计与导航策略、消除离散动作空间，BCNav缓解了域差距与运动学差距，为真实机器人声源导航提供了可部署的方案。

Abstract: The ability to navigate toward sound sources extends a robot's reach beyond its visual field, enabling response to auditory events in unknown environments. To equip robots with this capability, existing methods couple acoustic and visual information through joint audio-visual learning in acoustic simulators. However, acoustic simulation is both low-fidelity and expensive, producing a domain gap that prevents reliable real-world deployment, while the discrete action spaces inherited from grid-based simulators introduce an additional kinematic gap on physical robots. To alleviate these issues, we propose BCNav, a decoupled framework that separates the acoustic module from the learned navigation policy using direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation: an estimator provides a scalar bearing to the sound source, so the navigation policy only processes depth images and a bearing angle, two inputs whose domain gaps are well characterized. We collect shortest-path demonstrations with calibrated bearing noise injection and train the policy via imitation learning to output continuous velocity commands directly executable on ground robots. We demonstrate the method in simulation and on a physical robot, navigating unknown environments without any acoustic fine-tuning, prior mapping, or real-world audio data collection. Code is available at https://github.com/york1to/bcnav.

</details>


### [60] [Multifunctional Locomotion Control of Multi-Jointed BURs with Swimming and gait Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37086)
*Takumi Asada,Hideo Furuhashi,Kenta Tabata,Renato Miyagusuku,Koichi Ozaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态仿生水下机器人控制机制，利用同一多关节结构（四条腿鳍、各四轴）实现多种运动模式的表达，并通过势函数控制实现行为间切换，经物理与仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态仿生水下机器人需结合水生生物特性实现游泳与足式运动，但现有运动控制依赖基于规则的行为选择和设计者主观判断，限制了机器人获得预定范围之外的新行为能力。

Method: 提出一种能从同一多关节结构表达多模态运动能力的机构与控制系统。机构配置四条腿鳍，每条腿鳍具有四个轴。控制器基于传感器模态实现非线性行为，而非依赖预定义的条件规则式运动函数。通过多种传感器数据与行为模式进行物理与仿真测试，并采用势函数进行多模态运动控制。

Result: 验证了利用势函数进行多模态运动控制可实现两种或三种行为之间的切换；物理与仿真测试均验证了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 将控制方法实现为多模态控制器，有望增强其在水下探索中的应用。项目主页：https://tasada038.github.io/multi-jointed-bur/。

Abstract: Multimodal biomimetic underwater robots (BURs) can conduct underwater tasks suitable for the environment. Combining the characteristics of aquatic organisms enables the swimming and leggedlocomotion required for underwater exploration. Locomotion control mechanism relies on rule-based behavior selection and the designer's discretion. This limits the robot's ability to acquire new behavioral capabilities to the predetermined range of behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose a mechanism and control system that enables the expression of multimodal locomotion capabilities from the same multi-jointed structure. A mechanism equipped with four leg-fins each having four axes is used. This controller achieves nonlinear behavior based on sensor modalities, rather than relying on predefined conditional rule-based on locomotion functions. This system was validated through both physical and simulation testing based on multiple sensor data and behavioral patterns. Utilizing a potential function in multimodal locomotion control was verified to enable transitions between two or three behaviors. Implementing the control method as a multimodal controller is expected to enhance its application in underwater exploration. Our project page is at https://tasada038.github.io/multi-jointed-bur/.

</details>


### [61] [V2X-WAM: A Cooperative World Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37098)
*Junwei You,Weizhe Tang,Can Wang,Yan Zhao,Jun Hua,Haotian Shi,Wei Zhang,Lin Wang,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了V2X-WAM，一个车路协同世界动作模型，将协同场景理解、动作生成与未来世界推理紧密耦合，通过可靠性感知的时空表征、量化通信与闭环未来预测反馈来提升端到端协同驾驶的规划精度与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同驾驶方法主要利用路侧信息增强当前场景表征，却很少显式建模未来驾驶动作的后果，导致规划器难以预判决策与动态交通环境的交互。为实现更安全高效的端到端自动驾驶，需要将未来世界推理纳入协同规划闭环。

Method: 提出V2X-WAM：从车端与路侧观测构建可靠性感知的时空表征，并将路侧信息压缩为紧凑的量化消息以降低通信开销；基于协同表征，多模态规划器生成候选轨迹，该轨迹显式地作为未来占据与动态流预测的条件；将预测的未来世界后果反馈回规划器以精炼轨迹，形成动作与未来世界演化的闭环交互。

Result: 在大规模真实车路协同驾驶数据集上，V2X-WAM在规划精度和安全性上一致优于代表性端到端协同驾驶方法，同时实现更强的未来世界预测能力，并显著降低通信开销。消融实验进一步验证了各设计模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将协同感知、动作生成与未来世界推理闭环耦合，V2X-WAM证明了未来后果建模与高效路侧消息压缩在协同自动驾驶中的关键价值，为端到端协同驾驶提供了一种兼顾性能、安全与通信效率的有效范式。

Abstract: Vehicle-infrastructure cooperation can complement onboard sensing with broader and more informative observations of the traffic environment, providing valuable support for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing cooperative driving methods mainly exploit roadside information to enhance the representation of the current scene, while the future consequences of prospective driving actions are rarely modeled explicitly. This limits the ability of the planner to anticipate how its decisions may interact with the evolving traffic environment. To address this issue, we propose V2X-WAM, a cooperative world action model that tightly couples cooperative scene understanding, action generation, and future-world reasoning. V2X-WAM constructs a reliability-aware spatiotemporal representation from vehicle- and infrastructure-side observations, while compressing infrastructure information into a compact quantized message for efficient communication. Based on the resulting cooperative representation, a multimodal planner generates prospective trajectories, which explicitly condition future occupancy and dynamic-flow prediction. The predicted world consequences are then fed back to refine the planned trajectory, forming a closed interaction between action and future-world evolution. Experiments on a large-scale real-world cooperative driving dataset demonstrate that V2X-WAM consistently improves planning accuracy and safety over representative end-to-end cooperative driving methods, while achieving stronger future-world prediction and substantially lower communication overhead. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>


### [62] [Adversarially Robust Geometric Safety Certificates for Nonholonomic Robots Against Maneuvering Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37126)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Bazeela Banday,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗鲁棒几何证书AR-DPCBF，通过证书参数的闭式收缩将障碍物最坏机动影响编码进安全集几何，实现有界机动障碍物下的安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒控制障碍函数方法将障碍物动作视为一般扰动，保守且未利用结构；而微分博弈方法在线计算代价高，难以用于实时导航。因此需要一种兼顾鲁棒性与计算效率的方法。

Method: 利用视线(LoS)证书的结构性质：机器人与障碍物动作通过共同的、状态相关的几何增益进入证书，该增益在最坏情况比较中抵消，从而将微分博弈简化为障碍物机动能力与机器人纵/转向权限中较弱者的直接比较；在抛物线证书上实例化为AR-DPCBF，并用滑动窗口估计器为未知障碍物能力提供高概率上界，同时给出软约束与缓冲变体。

Result: 在运动学自行车动力学与有界输入下，建立收缩安全集对所有可行障碍物机动的前向不变性的充分条件；仿真表明在不同障碍物能力、密度与能力失配下，障碍物违规与碰撞大幅减少，并证明对障碍物导数的逐点鲁棒化无法替代几何收缩。

Conclusion: 通过闭式几何收缩而非逐点扰动处理，可在保持在线可行性的同时获得对抗鲁棒的安全保证；未知能力情形下可通过估计上界以相应覆盖概率保持保证。

Abstract: Safe navigation against obstacles that can actively maneuver within bounded capabilities remains challenging: robust control barrier function methods typically treat obstacle actions as generic disturbances, while differential-game approaches are computationally expensive for online navigation. We propose an adversarially robust geometric certificate that accounts for the worst-case effect of admissible obstacle maneuvers directly in the safe-set geometry through a closed-form contraction of the certificate parameters. The construction exploits a structural property of line-of-sight (LoS) certificates: the robot and obstacle actions enter the certificate through a common state-dependent geometric gain. This gain cancels in the worst-case comparison, reducing the differential game to a direct comparison between obstacle maneuvering capability and the weaker of the robot's longitudinal and steering authorities. Instantiated on the parabolic certificate, the construction yields Adversarially Robust Dynamic Parabolic Control Barrier Functions (AR-DPCBF), for which we establish sufficient conditions for forward invariance of the contracted safe set against all admissible obstacle maneuvers under kinematic bicycle dynamics with bounded inputs. When the obstacle capability is unknown, a sliding-window estimator supplies a high-probability upper bound, allowing the guarantee to be retained with the corresponding coverage probability. We further formulate soft and buffered variants to recover feasibility in dense environments. Simulations across obstacle capabilities, densities, and capability mismatch show substantial reductions in barrier violations and collisions and demonstrate that pointwise robustification of the barrier derivative cannot substitute for contraction of its geometry.

</details>


### [63] [ReF-HIL: Shaping the Critic around Human Action Neighborhoods for Efficient Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37131)
*Shaoyin Luo,Song Wang,Shibo Xia,Tianle Zhang,Zhaowei Liang,Guanghui Shen,Bin Wang,Dan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReF-HIL框架，通过人类参考引导的价值塑造与人类动作围栏，提升真实世界机器人操作HIL-RL的训练效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HIL-RL未充分利用成功的人类经验进行价值学习，导致真实世界训练成本高；同时持续的模仿惩罚限制了价值驱动的策略改进。

Method: ReF-HIL包含两部分：（1）人类参考引导价值塑造，从成功人类经验中学习独立的价值参考以指导在线价值学习，并融入局部纠正反馈；（2）人类动作围栏，学习人类动作邻域，在邻域内允许价值驱动优化且无模仿惩罚，在邻域外约束策略与价值更新。

Result: 在五个多样且具挑战性的真实世界操作任务上，ReF-HIL相比基线提升了整体学习效率与成功率；仅需18-63分钟主动训练即达90%自主成功率，最终成功率为91.7%-100%。

Conclusion: 结果展示了人类引导强化学习在真实世界中高效获取可靠操作技能的潜力。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) offers a promising route to efficient training of robotic manipulation policies by combining autonomous learning with human demonstrations and online corrections. However, insufficient use of successful human experience in value learning prolongs costly real-world training, while persistent imitation penalties can limit value-driven policy improvement. To address these limitations, we propose ReF-HIL, an efficient HIL-RL framework that uses human guidance to accelerate the learning process. Human-Reference-Guided Value Shaping learns an independent value reference from successful human experience to guide online value learning, while incorporating local corrective feedback. A Human Action Fence defines a learned human-action neighborhood, allowing value-driven optimization for better performance without imitation penalties inside while constraining policy and value updates outside. Experiments on five diverse and challenging real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved overall learning efficiency and higher success rates compared with the evaluated baselines. Specifically, ReF-HIL reaches 90% autonomous success in only 18-63 minutes of active training and achieves final success rates of 91.7-100%. These results highlight the potential of human-guided reinforcement learning to acquire reliable manipulation skills efficiently in the real world. Project website: https://anonymous.4open.science/w/ReF-HIL-7762/

</details>


### [64] [CoRe-VLA: Preserving Cross-View Coordination in VLAs under Camera Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37150)
*Tianhang Pan,Xuanhao Wang,Yiwen Pang,Bo Zhou,Jun Yang,Min-Ling Zhang,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe-VLA，一个无需额外多视角数据采集与VLA微调的即插即用框架，通过重建点云并从训练视角渲染观测，解决外部相机偏移下的跨视角协调崩溃问题，显著提升主流VLA在相机偏移下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA结合视觉语言表示与动作生成，实现语言引导控制，但在相机偏移下任务成功率大幅下降，限制可靠部署。现有方法需采集配对多视角观测并微调VLA或训练视觉适配模块，带来额外的数据采集与训练成本。作者识别出外部相机偏移下的“跨视角协调崩溃”：机器人过度依赖手腕视角线索，丢失全局视角后执行子任务顺序错误。

Method: 提出CoRe-VLA即插即用框架，无需额外多视角数据与VLA微调，可兼容现有VLA。它重建场景点云并从VLA训练视角渲染观测以恢复跨视角协调；其中Render-to-Camera (R2C) Restoration减少渲染引起的视觉退化，Execution-Trajectory-Conditioned Alignment (ETCA)减少机器人空闲时间并缓解异步执行中的运动冲突。

Result: 在5个真实机器人任务、LIBERO-100与LIBERO-Plus上实验表明，CoRe-VLA在相机偏移下大幅提升主流VLA任务成功率。例如真实环境中1.6m相机偏移下，PI0.5成功率从13.3%提升至83.3%。

Conclusion: CoRe-VLA通过从训练视角渲染点云恢复跨视角协调，无需额外数据或微调即可显著提升VLA在相机偏移下的鲁棒性与任务成功率，具备即插即用与广泛兼容性。

Abstract: VLAs combine pretrained vision-language representations with action generation to enable language-guided control across diverse tasks, becoming a mainstream paradigm in embodied intelligence. However, multiple studies have reported VLA's substantial declines in task success under camera shifts, revealing a key vulnerability that limits reliable deployment. To address this vulnerability, existing methods collect paired observations of the same scene from different viewpoints to fine-tune the VLA or train visual adaptation modules. Unfortunately, they require additional data collection and VLA training costs. In this paper, we first identify \emph{cross-view coordination breakdown} under external camera shifts: the robot may rely too heavily on wrist-view cues and consequently execute subtasks in the wrong order when losing global view. Motivated by this, we propose CoRe-VLA, a plug-and-play framework requiring neither additional multi-view data collection nor VLA fine-tuning, which can incorporate with exsiting VLAs. It reconstructs a scene point cloud and renders the observation from the VLA's training viewpoint to restore cross-view coordination. In CoRe-VLA, Render-to-Camera (R2C) Restoration reduces rendering-induced visual degradation, while Execution-Trajectory-Conditioned Alignment (ETCA) reduces robot idle time and mitigates motion conflicts during asynchronous execution. Experiments on 5 real-robot tasks, LIBERO-100 and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate CoRe-VLA substantially improves task success across mainstream VLAs under camera shifts. For example, CoRe-VLA raises PI0.5's success rate from 13.3% to 83.3% at a 1.6m camera shift in real-robot environment.

</details>


### [65] [Disentangling Spurious Correlations in Vision-Language-Action Models via Predicting Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37165)
*Junghyun Kim,Ngseo Kim,ChungWoo Lee,Seoyeon Lee,Woo-Jeong Baek,Adam Zhou,Chip Huyen,Jun-Ki Lee,Gi-Cheon Kang,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出领域不变潜在前瞻（DILL）表示学习框架，通过领域不变未来潜在表示监督策略，缓解VLA模型的捷径学习，提升视觉分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在视觉分布偏移下表现脆弱，往往依赖与领域特定因素相关的虚假关联，而非任务相关结构，因此需要缓解这种捷径学习。

Method: 训练任务-领域编码器，采用对比目标与高斯解耦正则化，将任务相关结构从领域特定的视觉变化中分离；该编码器通过前瞻预测与领域解耦为VLA策略学习提供未来潜在表示，促使策略关注任务相关结构。使用领域变换后的轨迹数据进行监督。

Result: 反事实任务视角评估表明DILL减少了对捷径的依赖；LIBERO-Plus评估显示视觉鲁棒性提升，平均成功率69.1%，比最强基线高11.4个百分点；真实世界操作实验进一步验证其适用性。潜在空间诊断显示，行为增益伴随着更好地保留任务一致结构、抑制领域特定变化的表示。

Conclusion: DILL能有效缓解VLA策略的捷径学习，提升视觉鲁棒性，并在仿真和真实场景中均具有适用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remain brittle under visual distribution shifts, often relying on spurious correlations tied to domain-specific factors rather than task-relevant structure. We propose Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead (DILL), a representation-learning framework that mitigates shortcut learning in VLA policies. Our key idea is to supervise policies with domain-invariant future latents learned from domain-transformed trajectory data. A Task-Domain Encoder is trained with contrastive objectives and Gaussian disentanglement regularization to separate task-relevant structure from domain-specific visual variation. The learned encoder then provides future latents for VLA policy learning through lookahead prediction and domain disentanglement, encouraging the policy to focus on task-relevant structure rather than incidental visual factors. Counterfactual task-view evaluations show that DILL reduces shortcut reliance, while LIBERO-Plus evaluations demonstrate improved visual robustness, with 69.1% average success, 11.4 percentage points above the strongest baseline. Real-world manipulation experiments further support DILL's applicability beyond controlled simulation. Complementary latent-space diagnostics show that these behavioral gains are accompanied by representations that better preserve task-consistent structure while suppressing domain-specific variation. Our project page is available at https://dill-vla.github.io/.

</details>


### [66] [EgoHumanoid-V2: Human-to-Humanoid Transfer of Coordinated Whole-Body Skills for Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37181)
*Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Chongyang Xu,Modi Shi,Shijia Peng,Li Chen,Tianyu Li,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Steven Hoi,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EgoHumanoid-V2，首个面向协调全身移动操作的第一人称人类到人形机器人的技能迁移框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类示范能捕获多样场景与丰富全身技能，且无需机器人遥操作。既有自我中心迁移研究多聚焦解耦控制下的场景泛化，对协调全身技能的直接迁移探索不足。

Method: 核心为粗到细的动作对齐，结合运动学参考修正与动力学感知的精细化，在保留全身协调的同时提升末端执行器位姿精度；并采用机械臂渲染与训练时图像增强，缩小视觉具身差距、提升视角鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个真实任务上，基于对齐后人类数据训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略实现零样本技能迁移，无需目标任务机器人示范，任务得分与遥操作数据训练的策略相当，且采集成本更低。

Conclusion: 结果支持将人类数据作为直接的技能监督信号，用于人形机器人的全身移动操作技能学习。

Abstract: Human demonstrations capture diverse scenes and rich whole-body skills without requiring robot teleoperation. Prior work on egocentric transfer has emphasized scene generalization in loco-manipulation under decoupled control, leaving direct transfer of coordinated whole-body skills less explored. We present EgoHumanoid-V2, the first egocentric human-to-humanoid skill transfer framework for coordinated whole-body loco-manipulation. At its core, coarse-to-fine action alignment combines kinematic reference correction with dynamics-aware refinement. It improves end-effector pose accuracy while preserving whole-body coordination. We also use robot-arm rendering and training-time image augmentation to reduce the visual embodiment gap and improve viewpoint robustness. On four real-world tasks, vision-language-action (VLA) policies trained on aligned human data show zero-shot skill transfer without target-task robot demonstrations. Task scores are comparable to those of policies trained on teleoperation data at a lower collection cost. These results support human data as direct skill supervision.

</details>


### [67] [Recovering the View: Benchmarking Physical Active Vision for Occlusion Recovery in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37292)
*Kaijun Luo,Yudi Huang,Qijun Zhong,Xinshuai Song,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出双臂主动视觉基准 BAVO-Bench（用 Clean、阶段遮挡、随机时刻遮挡三种条件系统控制可见性），并基于此提出主动视觉策略 A-FAR，将移动相机观测统一到机器人中心3D坐标系，从预训练4D模型中蒸馏关系结构及其未来演化，实现未来感知的几何引导，提升对遮挡的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人主动视觉允许在任务相关观测不可靠时改变视角，但现有操作基准对"策略如何在执行中从遮挡中恢复"的支持有限，缺乏系统评估主动视觉恢复能力的平台。

Method: 构建 BAVO-Bench 双臂主动视觉基准，通过 Clean、Stage Occlusion、Random-time Occlusion 三种条件控制外部可见性；提出 A-FAR 策略，将移动相机观测表示在统一的机器人中心3D坐标系中，并从预训练4D模型蒸馏关系结构及其未来演化，为策略提供未来感知的几何引导，且部署时无需未来观测。

Result: 在多个操作任务上，A-FAR 提升了对结构化遮挡和时间偏移遮挡的鲁棒性，同时在干净观测下仍保持较强性能。

Conclusion: BAVO-Bench 为双臂主动视觉下的遮挡恢复研究提供了系统化评测支持，A-FAR 通过未来感知几何引导实现了视角与操作联合控制，验证了主动视觉恢复的有效性。

Abstract: Physical active vision allows robots to change their viewpoint when task-relevant observations become unreliable, yet existing manipulation benchmarks provide limited support for studying how policies recover from occlusion during execution. We introduce BAVO-Bench (Bimanual Active Vision under Occlusion), a bimanual active-vision benchmark that systematically controls external visibility through Clean, Stage Occlusion, and Random-time Occlusion conditions, enabling evaluation of both manipulation performance and active visual recovery. Building on this setting, we present A-FAR (Active Future-Aware Recovery), an active-vision policy for joint viewpoint and manipulation control. A-FAR represents moving-camera observations in a unified robot-centric 3D frame and distills relational structure together with its future evolution from a pretrained 4D model, providing the policy with future-aware geometric guidance without requiring future observations at deployment. Experiments across multiple manipulation tasks show that A-FAR improves robustness to both structured and temporally shifted occlusions while maintaining strong performance under clean observations.

</details>


### [68] [Remember What You Did: Action-History Memory with Dual-Expert Denoising for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37307)
*Yaxin Zhao,Dianye Huang,Chenwei Wang,Chenguang Yang,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActMem-VLA，通过冻结基座 VLA、仅训练 Mamba 记忆模块与轻量 PreAction Expert，实现历史条件化的动作去噪引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 缺乏对交互历史的显式访问，易受感知混叠影响，导致相似观测下动作歧义与成功率下降。已有方法需联合微调记忆模块与基座 VLA，带来额外策略训练成本，故需将可训练的历史条件引导与冻结基座策略细化分离。

Method: 双专家交接架构：Mamba 记忆模块将已执行动作历史编码为记忆，与当前上下文共同条件化轻量 PreAction Expert（PAE）。PAE 在高噪声去噪早期引导任务推进，再将部分去噪动作交给冻结的 Action Expert（AE）在低噪声阶段细化细节。训练中基座 VLA 全程冻结，仅联合优化 Mamba 与 PAE。

Result: LIBERO-Mem 上十个任务平均成功率 80.8%，优于 π0.5 的 65.2% 与 MemoryVLA 的 49.5%，仅增加 3.45% 参数；四个真实世界任务上较 π0.5 平均成功率提升 28.8%。

Conclusion: 通过冻结基座策略、仅训练历史记忆与引导模块，可低成本有效缓解感知混叠，提升长程操作任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have driven rapid progress in robotic manipulation, demonstrating strong fine-grained control and promising performance on long-horizon tasks. However, many existing VLAs lack explicit access to interaction history, making them vulnerable to perceptual aliasing: similar current observations and robot states at different task stages may induce action ambiguity and lower success rate. Existing methods incorporate temporal or progress cues through feature conditioning, action-prior modification, or sampling guidance. However, methods that jointly fine-tune memory modules and the base VLA incur additional policy-training costs, motivating the separation of trainable history-conditioned steering from frozen base-policy refinement. We propose ActMem-VLA, a dual-expert handover architecture that augments a frozen, fine-tuned VLA with a memory plugin comprising a Mamba-based memory module and a lightweight PreAction Expert (PAE). Specifically, Mamba encodes executed-action history into memory that conditions PAE alongside current context. With these inputs, PAE steers task progression during early, high-noise denoising, then passes the partially denoised action to the frozen Action Expert (AE) to refine action details during the remaining low-noise steps. The fine-tuned base VLA remains frozen throughout training, while only the Mamba module and PAE are jointly optimized. On LIBERO-Mem, ActMem-VLA achieves 80.8\% average success across all ten tasks, compared with 65.2\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 49.5\% for MemoryVLA, while introducing only 3.45\% additional parameters. Across four real-world tasks, it improves the average success rate over $π_{0.5}$ by 28.8\%.

</details>


### [69] [Battery-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Aggressive Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37316)
*Alejandro Sanchez Roncero,Olov Andersson,Petter Ogren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究在学习型控制器中利用电池电压信息扩展推力指令范围，从而在激进飞行任务中提升性能，并在Crazyflie Brushless实机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机竞速、追逃等敏捷飞行任务需要强加速与精确转向，但电池放电和负载下电压下降会导致可用推力变化。保守的指令限制虽能容忍这种变化，却牺牲了未使用的性能。作者希望让学习型控制器既能利用额外推力，又能保留飞控的电压补偿与角速率控制。

Method: 训练模拟器将辨识出的负载瞬态电池模型与旋翼动力学及固件饱和特性耦合；前馈策略在训练和部署阶段均接收滤波后的电压信号；通过受控消融实验区分更大推力指令范围与电压信息各自的贡献。

Result: 在38 g Crazyflie Brushless上，相比原始权限的RL，策略在3.84 m/s时圆形跟踪误差降低49%，同时保持简单任务精度；20圈平均竞速时间从106.22 s降至95.24 s。与同样增加权限但无电压信息的策略相比，硬件误差和竞速时间分别降低15.3%和4.5%。仿真中，将策略电压输入替换为不同电池状态的记录会恶化困难圆形跟踪，竞速中影响较小但依赖电压。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在激进学习飞行中，简单电压输入可对现有执行器补偿形成有效补充，兼顾性能提升与精度保持。

Abstract: Agile flight tasks such as drone racing and pursuit-evasion require strong acceleration and precise turns, but the available thrust changes as the battery discharges and voltage drops under load. Conservative command limits make this variation easier to tolerate, at the cost of unused performance. We investigate how learned controllers can use that additional thrust while retaining the flight controller's voltage compensation and rate control. Our training simulator couples an identified load-transient battery model to rotor dynamics and firmware saturation. The feedforward policy receives filtered voltage during both training and deployment. Controlled ablations distinguish the benefit of a larger thrust-command range from that of voltage information. On a 38 g Crazyflie Brushless, the resulting policy reduces circle tracking error by 49% relative to stock-authority RL at 3.84 m/s, while preserving easy-task precision. Mean 20-lap race time decreases from 106.22 s to 95.24 s. Compared with a voltage-blind policy with the same increased authority, hardware error and race time are lower by 15.3% and 4.5%, respectively. In simulation, replacing the policy's voltage input with a recording from a different battery condition worsens hard-circle tracking, with a smaller, voltage-dependent effect in racing. Together, these results show where a simple voltage input complements existing actuator compensation in aggressive learned flight.

</details>


### [70] [Taming VLAs under Robot Execution Errors: Self-Compensation and Stress Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37334)
*Sohyun Lee,Yoonjae Baek,Jaesang Won,Jinnyeong Kim,Kang Hyunwoo,Seung-Hwan Baek,Ivan Laptev,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自补偿VLA（self-compensating VLA），一种部署时自适应方法，利用指令动作与实际执行动作之间的残差在线更新策略，使VLA预先补偿执行误差；并构建RoboStress仿真基准，在仿真与两款实体机械臂上均显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在机器人实际执行动作偏离指令动作时往往失败，这类执行误差源于机械磨损、负载变化等机器人本体与工况因素。现有方法难以覆盖这些部署条件，且缺乏可控的测试手段（仅靠实体机器人测试不现实）。

Method: 提出self-compensating VLA：无需任务奖励或标签，在部署时用VLA指令动作与机器人实际执行动作之间的残差对策略进行在线更新，从而在生成指令时预先补偿执行误差。同时构建RoboStress可控仿真基准，将摩擦、回隙（backlash）、柔顺性、重力补偿误差等关节级模型组合成七种部署场景，其执行误差依赖机器人状态与运动历史。
