Built on nuPlan, doPlan contains 5,154 human-written passenger instructions spanning 169.1 hours of cumulative instruction-aligned context over 50.9 hours of unique driving, with annotation windows ranging from 30.0 to 508.8 s. The annotations capture immediate, deferred, event-conditioned, persistent, and multi-stage passenger intent. The dataset, annotation interface, and supporting resources are publicly available at https://github.com/Mi3-Lab/doPlan. We evaluate four language-conditioned driving models and find that sensitivity to passenger language does not reliably translate into behavior consistent with the requested direction. More broadly, among 2,161 examples with a matched future maneuver, the first associated maneuver occurs a median of 24.6 s after the evaluation point, and only 9.8% occur within the models' common 5 s prediction horizon. These findings highlight the need to connect persistent passenger intent with successive planning decisions. doPlan provides a setting for studying how unresolved goals can be retained, grounded in evolving scenes, and tracked across multiple stages, including how a planner determines when a future goal becomes relevant to the current plan.

</details>


### [105] [EgoAlign: Bridging the Human-Humanoid Gap for Long-Range Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38046)
*Yiming Jiang,Chen Jin,Chongyang Xu,Yilun Chen,Aimin Hao,Yisheng He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoAlign 是一个数据构建框架，将第一视角人类演示转化为与通用连续全身控制器兼容的动作与状态监督，无需采集真机演示，仅用适配后的人类数据微调 VLA 模型即可零样本部署到实体人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角人类演示是易获取的任务经验来源，但身体尺度差异、控制器响应不同以及缺失机器人状态，限制了其作为人形机器人训练监督的价值。

Method: 利用目标机器人模型与仿真器，通过执行反馈引导演示采集；保留视觉引导周期性迈步的运动参考，同时通过尺度对齐与控制器在环细化适配上半身交互几何；最后用因果重放重建对应的机器人状态与运动 token 标签，与人类观测配对用于训练。

Result: 仅用适配后的人类演示微调视觉-语言-动作模型，并零样本部署到实体人形机器人，策略可完成长距离物体搬运、导航至未见目标位置以及独立评估的足部交互；相比纯运动学对齐，细化提升了仿真手部对齐与实体抓取成功率；人类采集相比遥操作减少了现场采集时间。

Conclusion: EgoAlign 能在不采集真机演示的情况下，把人类第一视角演示转化为有效的人形机器人训练监督，为通用全身控制器的数据构建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Egocentric human demonstrations offer an accessible source of task experience, but differences in body scale and controller response, together with missing robot states, limit their value as humanoid training supervision. We present EgoAlign, a data-construction framework that converts these demonstrations into action and state supervision compatible with a general-purpose, continuous whole-body controller, without collecting physical-robot demonstrations. Using the target-robot model and simulator, EgoAlign guides demonstration collection through execution feedback. It preserves locomotion references for visually guided periodic stepping while adapting upper-body interaction geometry through scale alignment and controller-in-the-loop refinement. A final causal replay reconstructs the corresponding robot states and motion-token labels for training with the human observations. We assess the resulting supervision by fine-tuning a vision--language--action model solely on adapted human demonstrations and deploying it zero-shot on a physical humanoid. The resulting policies perform long-range object relocation, navigation to unseen goal positions, and independently evaluated foot interaction. Refinement improves simulated hand alignment and physical pickup success over kinematic alignment alone, while human collection reduces on-site acquisition time relative to teleoperation. https://lambdahumanoid.github.io/EgoAlign/

</details>


### [106] [A QCQP-Representable IMU Pre-Integration Factor for Certifiable State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38048)
*Utkarsh Rai,Zhexin Xu,Bang-Shien Chen,David Rosen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个可QCQP表示的IMU预积分因子，实现了带预积分惯性测量的可认证估计，并将其应用于GNSS-IMU平滑问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性传感是机器人领域中常见且可靠的模态，将其纳入可认证估计可拓宽其实用范围。然而，标准IMU预积分依赖指数映射，无法给出精确多项式表示；同时QCQP形式需要辅助提升变量，导致标准SDP松弛可能过于松散。

Method: 基于Cayley映射推导IMU预积分因子以获得所需代数结构，并引入一组显式冗余约束以收紧所得松弛，从而构建可QCQP表示且松弛较紧的预积分因子。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上应用于可认证GNSS-IMU平滑问题，结果表明所提形式可获得紧松弛，并将估计问题求解到经认证的全局最优。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将IMU预积分直接引入可认证估计，验证了基于Cayley映射与冗余约束的QCQP表述在紧松弛和全局最优认证方面的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a QCQP-representable IMU pre-integration factor that enables certifiable estimation with pre-integrated inertial measurements. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to directly incorporate IMU pre-integration into certifiable estimation. Inertial sensing is a common and reliable modality in robotics, and incorporating it broadens the practical scope of certifiable estimation. The main challenges are obtaining the required algebraic structure and a sufficiently tight convex relaxation. Standard IMU pre-integration relies on the exponential map, which does not admit an exact polynomial representation. Moreover, obtaining a QCQP formulation requires auxiliary lifting variables, for which the standard semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation can be loose. We address these issues by deriving an IMU pre-integration factor based on the Cayley map and an explicit set of redundant constraints that tighten the resulting relaxation. To validate the proposed factor, we apply it to certifiable GNSS-IMU smoothing and evaluate it on synthetic and real-world data. The results show that the proposed formulation yields tight relaxations and solves the resulting estimation problems to verified global optimality.

</details>


### [107] [Pow3R-SLAM: Real-Time RGB-D SLAM with 3D Reconstruction Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38054)
*Christopher Kolios,Ishaan Mehta,Sasa Janjic,Yeganeh Bahoo,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pow3R-SLAM：一种实时RGB-D SLAM系统，将深度作为先验融入双视图3D重建，性能优于MASt3R-SLAM和ORB-SLAM3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RGB-D SLAM系统在深度图像稀疏时表现不佳。作者受MASt3R-SLAM（使用双视图3D重建先验的单目SLAM）启发，希望将深度作为网络预测的先验而非用于融合的几何信息，从而获得条件更好的点图并推断空白区域的深度。

Method: 提出Pow3R-SLAM，利用Pow3R进行跟踪与建图。将可用深度作为网络预测的先验，借助双视图光度、深度和内参数据推断空白区域的深度，并引入一种混合变体以提升速度。

Result: 在TUM、7-Scenes和Replica共24条序列上按MASt3R-SLAM协议评测：运行速度快1.6倍，平均轨迹误差低15%，未缩放误差低3.1倍，地图更稠密（Chamfer距离低30%）。混合变体比MASt3R-SLAM快2.1倍，达25.3 FPS，同时保持更高的跟踪与建图精度。相比RGB-D模式的ORB-SLAM3，在TUM、7-Scenes和ETH3D-SLAM上更准确，并完成全部TUM序列。

Conclusion: 除少数自相似场景外，整体性能表明将深度作为双视图3D重建SLAM的先验是有益的。项目网页已提供，代码将在接收后开源。

Abstract: We present Pow3R-SLAM, a real-time RGB-D simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system that uses Pow3R for tracking and mapping. Inspired by MASt3R-SLAM, a recent work on monocular SLAM using two-view 3D reconstruction priors, we extend the work to incorporate depth as a prior on the network's prediction, rather than as geometry to fuse. Where traditional RGB-D SLAM systems struggle with sparsity in the depth images, Pow3R utilizes the available depth to give a better-conditioned pointmap, while inferring the depths in empty regions from the two-view photometric, depth, and intrinsic data. Evaluated against MASt3R-SLAM following its protocol on 24 sequences from TUM, 7-Scenes, and Replica, Pow3R-SLAM runs 1.6x faster in wall time, has 15% lower mean trajectory error, a 3.1x lower unscaled error, and produces denser maps, with a 30% lower Chamfer distance. We also introduce a hybrid variant that runs 2.1x faster than MASt3R-SLAM at 25.3 frames per second (FPS), while maintaining improved tracking and mapping accuracy. Against ORB-SLAM3 in RGB-D mode, Pow3R-SLAM is more accurate on TUM, 7-Scenes, and ETH3D-SLAM, and completes every TUM sequence. While Pow3R-SLAM can struggle on a small set of self-similar scenes, its overall performance shows that adding depth as a prior for two-view 3D reconstruction SLAM can be beneficial. A project webpage is available at: https://ChrisKolios.github.io/Pow3R-SLAM , and code will be made open-source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [108] [WorldLine: Action-Driven Visual Simulation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38059)
*Shenghe Zheng,Wenbo Li,Jiyao Zhang,Bin Xia,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldLine 是一个动作驱动的视觉模拟器，将可迁移动力学学习与异构动作 grounding 解耦，基于上万小时无动作机器人视频与多本体动作数据训练，实现高效因果 rollout 与策略评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人学习受限于经验采集与候选行为评估的成本。视频生成模型可作为视觉模拟器在物理执行前预测动作结果，但现有方法往往偏向视觉逼真而忽视动作跟随准确性及机器人-物体动力学一致性；动作条件模拟器又依赖稀缺、本体特定的数据，难以在不同控制空间间共享。

Method: WorldLine 将可迁移动力学学习与异构动作 grounding 解耦：从超过 10,000 小时无动作机器人视频中学习操作动力学，并用超过 2,000 小时、跨十余种本体的动作轨迹进行 grounding。采用图像空间动作表示作为跨本体共享控制接口，结合多视角与失败增强训练及关系正则化提升交互敏感预测，并通过面向机器人的少步蒸馏实现高效因果 rollout，同时保留动作关键运动。

Result: 在留出与域外设置下，WorldLine 保持较强视觉质量与机器人运动一致性；在失败轨迹上，机器人掩膜 IoU 相比最强基线提升 0.1626。在 RoboTwin 与 AgiBot 上以 74% 平均准确率预测轨迹成功，比最强基线高一个百分点。在无 RoboTwin 训练或适配情况下，其 rollout 使任务成功率相比直接策略执行最高提升 21.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: WorldLine 兼具可扩展性与高效性，可作为视觉模拟器用于策略评估与具身规划。

Abstract: Real-world robot learning is constrained by the cost of collecting experience and evaluating candidate behaviors. Video generation models offer a scalable foundation for visual simulators that predict action outcomes before physical execution. Yet they often favor visual plausibility over accurate action following and coherent robot--object dynamics, while action-conditioned simulators depend on scarce, embodiment-specific data that are difficult to share across incompatible control spaces. We introduce WorldLine, an action-driven visual simulator that decouples transferable dynamics learning from heterogeneous action grounding. WorldLine learns manipulation dynamics from more than 10,000 hours of action-free robot videos and grounds them using over 2,000 hours of action trajectories across more than ten embodiments. An image-space action representation provides a shared control interface across embodiments, while multi-view and failure-enriched training with relational regularization improves interaction-sensitive prediction. Robot-focused few-step distillation enables efficient causal rollout while preserving action-critical motion. Across held-out and out-of-domain settings, WorldLine maintains strong visual quality and robot-motion agreement; on failed trajectories, it improves robot-mask IoU by 0.1626 over the strongest baseline. It predicts trajectory success with 74% mean accuracy across RoboTwin and AgiBot, one percentage point above the strongest baseline. Without RoboTwin training or adaptation, its rollouts improve task success by up to 21.4 percentage points over direct policy execution. Together, these capabilities make WorldLine a scalable and efficient visual simulator for policy evaluation and embodied planning. More results are available at \href{https://zhengsh123.github.io/WorldLine/}{project page}.

</details>


### [109] [MotorMind: Scaffolding General Vision Language Models for Zero-Shot Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38078)
*Bingxuan Li,Siqi Song,Yizhuo Wu,Jiarui Yao,Tong Zhang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MotorMind，将通用 VLM 的中层动作提议与确定性机器人控制、异步监控和后台记忆更新相连，在无需专门策略训练、编码智能体或额外接地图工具的情况下实现有效的零样本机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型零样本泛化能力有限，且依赖专用训练，难以直接受益于快速发展的通用 VLM；而现有 agentic 机器人系统虽借助 VLM 做高层推理，但依赖大量外部模型和工具，带来额外复杂性与成本。作者因此追问：通用 VLM 能否像人类遥操作者一样，直接从观测推理、发出动作并持续适应执行反馈，而不依赖外部模型或 SAM3 等接地图工具。

Method: 本文提出 MotorMind，一个机器人操作 harness：把 VLM 提议的中层动作连接到确定性机器人控制与反馈，并配备异步监控和后台记忆更新机制。整个方案不使用任务专用策略训练、编码智能体或额外接地工具。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 基础套件上达到 66.7% 成功率，扰动下为 53.8%，而所评估的先前零样本方法分别最多为 13.3% 和 19.2%。同一接口在真实 xArm6 机器人上，跨直接操作与人类扰动设置平均成功率达 95%。替换更强 VLM 主干可进一步提升性能，且剩余失败（主要来自视觉接地、具身推理和动作知识）随 VLM 能力提升而减少。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通用 VLM 在配备合适的中层动作表示和异步执行 harness 后，能够完成有效的零样本机器人操作。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation, but their zero-shot generalization in new tasks and environments remains limited, and their reliance on specialized training keeps them from benefiting directly from rapidly advancing general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs). In parallel, recent agentic robotic systems leverage VLMs for high-level reasoning or coding agents for robot control, but often depend on extensive external models and tools, introducing additional complexity and cost. This motivates us to ask: Can a general-purpose VLM itself operate a robot more like the human teleoperator by reasoning directly from observations, issuing actions, and continuously adapting to execution feedback, without relying on external models such as learned action experts, coding agents or grounding tools like SAM3? In this work, we introduce MotorMind, a robot manipulation harness that connects VLM-proposed mid-level actions to deterministic robot control and feedback, with asynchronous monitoring and background memory updates. Without task-specific policy training, coding agents, or additional grounding tools such as SAM3, MotorMind achieves 66.7% success on the base LIBERO-PRO suites and 53.8% under perturbations, compared with at most 13.3% and 19.2%, respectively, for the prior zero-shot methods we evaluate. The same interface reaches 95% average success on a real xArm6 robot across direct manipulation and human-perturbation settings. Replacing the backbone with a stronger VLM further improves performance, while the remaining failures - primarily due to visual grounding, embodied reasoning, and action knowledge - decrease as VLM capability improves. These results show that a general-purpose VLM, when equipped with an appropriate mid-level action representation and asynchronous execution harness, can perform effective zero-shot robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [110] [CrossBFM: Distilling a Shared Latent Behavior Space Across Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38087)
*Tan-Dzung Do,Tuan Dat Phuong,Nico Bohlinger,Cuc T. Trinh,Siwei Ju,Vien Anh Ngo,Jan Peters,Xinchao Wang,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrossBFM 通过将潜空间视为可跨本体迁移的资产，提出无机器人专属参数的统一编码器架构，将行为基础模型的蒸馏过程从数百 GPU 小时降至不足一 GPU 小时，并可跨多个类人机器人共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（BFM）可让类人机器人通过单一潜向量实现运动模仿、姿态到达或奖励最大化的提示式策略，但现有 Forward-Backward 表示方法为单个机器人训练需耗费数百 GPU 小时；且为第二个机器人重复训练会得到互不相关的潜空间，导致潜表示与本体绑定、无法统一或迁移。

Method: 基于重定向（retargeting）提供的帧级跨本体对应关系，提出统一编码器架构（无机器人专属参数），同时蒸馏所有训练本体的行为空间；随后用潜变量条件化跟踪器，通过常规 PPO 训练将蒸馏潜变量转化为全身控制。

Result: 在三个蒸馏类人机器人上三种提示模式均可迁移：运动跟踪中潜条件策略仅比关节条件策略损失 0.025 rad；姿态间平滑目标到达无摔倒；全部 41 个奖励提示均可优化。实验表明：编码器仅在四分之一运动语料上回归只损失 5% 跟踪性能；在机器人子集上训练、在未见机器人上评估可恢复至已见机器人 89% 的跟踪性能，体现对形态相似机器人的跨本体泛化。还验证了真实机器人上全部三种提示模式及基于 flow 生成潜变量的流程。

Conclusion: CrossBFM 以跨本体可迁移的潜空间为核心，大幅降低训练成本（蒸馏 <1 GPU 小时，跟踪 +10 GPU 小时），实现多本体统一的提示式行为控制，并展现出良好的数据效率与跨本体泛化能力，且在真实机器人上得到验证。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) give humanoids a promptable policy over a latent behavior space, enabling one single vector to represent a motion to imitate, a pose to reach, or a reward to maximize. Forward-Backward representations successfully produce such spaces, but at the cost of hundreds of GPU-hours for a single robot. Moreover, when the training process is repeated for a second robot, it produces a second space unrelated to the first, resulting in embodiment-specific latents that do not unify or transfer. We address these problems with CrossBFM, treating the latent space as the transferable asset for various embodiments. As retargeting provides frame-level cross-embodiment correspondence, we propose a unified encoder architecture with no robot-specific parameters for distilling the behavior space to address all training embodiments simultaneously in less than a GPU-hour. Following this encoder, latent-conditioned trackers turn the distilled latent into whole-body control in a conventional PPO training manner in just 10 more GPU-hours. On three distilled humanoids, all three prompting modes transfer: motion tracking with latent-conditioned policy losing only $0.025$ rad to its joint-conditioned counterpart, smooth goal reaching between poses with no falls, and reward optimization for all $41$ reward prompts. Our experiments further reveal that 1) regressing the encoder on a quarter of the motion corpus costs only $5\%$ of tracking performance and 2) training the encoder on a subset of robots and evaluating on an unseen one recovers up to $89\%$ of the tracking performance of seen robots, demonstrating cross-embodiment generalization to morphologically similar robots. We also verify the pipeline on real robots across all three prompting modes and with flow-based generated latents. Project website: https://dotandung.github.io/crossbfm/

</details>


### [111] [FORM: Robot Manipulation through Direct Material Law Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38105)
*Stepan Tretiakov,Ruihan Zhao,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Xingjian Li,Adam Thorpe,Hassan Iqbal,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FORM通过单次机器人交互在线识别可变形材料属性，识别时间从10-25分钟缩短至2-5秒，并可复用模型进行新运动与几何下的操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在与陌生可变形材料交互时缺乏其物理属性及力/运动响应先验知识，因此需要快速在线识别以保证可靠操作。

Method: 利用弱形式动量平衡，将观测到的材料运动与接触力转化为关于未知材料参数的线性方程；这些方程采用与正向仿真器相同的物质点法（MPM）离散化组装，因此识别问题化简为线性最小二乘求解，解可直接用于预测，无需重新拟合或转换。

Result: 在四类材料上，识别时间由迭代基线的约10-25分钟降至2-5秒，且在新运动、初始条件和几何上保持有竞争力精度；仿真与硬件上完成四项任务：弹性杆插入、弹性球杆高尔夫推杆、弹塑性塑形、目标体积倾倒；弹性属性误差在3.4%内，弹塑性属性误差在2%内，面团塑形IoU达72.4-77.8%，60-160 mL目标体积下平均倾倒误差3.8 mL。

Conclusion: FORM可从单次交互中高效、准确地识别材料属性，并将所得模型直接复用于新动作与新几何下的操作规划，兼具速度与精度。

Abstract: When interacting with an unfamiliar deformable material, a robot lacks prior knowledge of its physical properties and how it will respond to applied forces and motion. Rapid online identification is therefore essential for reliable manipulation. We present FORM (From Observed Response to Material laws), which identifies material properties from a single robot interaction and reuses the recovered model to plan manipulation under new actions and geometries. We use weak-form momentum balance to convert observed material motion and contact forces into linear equations in the unknown material parameters. These equations are assembled using the same material point method discretization as the forward simulator, so identification reduces to linear least-squares solves whose solutions can be used directly for prediction without refitting or conversion. Across four material classes, FORM reduces identification time from roughly 10--25 minutes for iterative baselines to 2--5 seconds, while maintaining competitive accuracy on new motions, initial conditions, and geometries. We demonstrate our approach in simulation and on hardware across four manipulation tasks: elastic rod insertion, golf putting with an elastic club, elastoplastic shaping, and target-volume pouring. In each task, the model identified from a single interaction is reused to plan new motions or manipulate a different geometry. FORM estimates elastic properties within 3.4% and elastoplastic properties within 2%, achieves 72.4--77.8% IoU in dough shaping, and keeps mean pouring error at 3.8 mL across target volumes of 60--160 mL.

</details>


### [112] [Rho: A Foundation for Efficiently Adaptable VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38164)
*Rho Team,Simran Bagaria,Daphne Chen,Dean Fortier,Jianlong Fu,Michael Harrison,Tess Hellebrekers,Neel Joshi,Andrey Kolobov,Dalton Moore,Galen Mullins,Michael Murray,Eduardo Salinas,Reuben Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rho是一系列面向双臂操作的开源权重VLA模型，在三种代表性双臂机器人本体上实现数据轻量化的任务适配，并具备基于纠正反馈的在线适配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用物理AI模型需兼具广泛视觉语言能力与精确的跨本体控制能力，同时要能高效适配下游任务。然而现有开源VLA模型在多本体部署和数据高效适配方面仍存在不足。

Method: 提出Rho系列开源权重VLA模型，面向双臂操作，覆盖YAM Box、UR AI Trainer、FR3 Duo三种本体。系统消融其动作专家架构与训练配方，采用本体中期训练（embodiment midtraining）；并引入内部潜在策略，从纠正反馈中学习为冻结的流匹配动作专家选择观测条件噪声输入。

Result: 对照仿真与真机实验表明，本体中期训练可提升下游适配效果；Rho各变体在评估任务、本体与基线中达到匹配或超越现有开源VLA模型的最强整体性能；仅需15个纠正回合即可让轻量模块实现分布边缘情形的在线适配。

Conclusion: Rho既是强通用机器人操作模型，也是实用的适配基础。作者开源基础模型与各本体专用检查点，以支持研究实验与工业落地。

Abstract: General-purpose physical AI models must combine broad visual and linguistic capabilities with precise control across robot embodiments and efficient adaptation to downstream tasks. We introduce Rho, a family of open-weights VLA models for bimanual manipulation designed for data-light task adaptation on 3 embodiments representative of dual-arm robots across research labs and the industry -- YAM Box, UR AI Trainer, and FR3 Duo. We systematically ablate Rho's action-expert architecture and training recipe, and show in controlled simulation and physical-robot experiments that embodiment midtraining improves downstream adaptation. The resulting Rho variants for YAM Box, UR AI Trainer, and FR3 Duo match or outperform existing open-weights VLAs and achieve the strongest overall performance across the tasks, embodiments, and baselines evaluated in this report. We further demonstrate the Rho model family's built-in capacity for online adaptation: an internal latent policy learns from corrective feedback to select observation-conditioned noise inputs for the frozen flow-matching action expert. With as few as 15 corrected episodes, adapting this lightweight module enables Rho to handle task situations at the fringe of its offline finetuning distribution. Together, these results position Rho as both a strong general-purpose robotic manipulation model and a practical foundation for adaptation. We release the base Rho model and the embodiment-specific checkpoints to facilitate Rho's deployment in research experiments and practical industrial use cases.

</details>


### [113] [Counterfactual Video Generation Enables Scalable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38172)
*Zihan Wang,Zhen Wu,Pieter Abbeel,Rocky Duan,Jitendra Malik,Carmelo Sferrazza,C. Karen Liu,Guanya Shi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一个real-to-sim-to-real框架，通过少量真实视频扩增出大量多样化训练数据，训练单一策略实现人形机器人搬运多类物体的泛化能力，并可在真实机器人上零微调部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过视觉模仿让人形机器人学习搬运物体等loco-manipulation技能是通向通用机器人的有前景路径，但采集多样化、高质量（全身可见、无遮挡交互）的交互视频成本高、难度大，成为规模化应用的实际障碍。

Method: PRISM先将少量真实视频通过视频到视频（V2V）生成扩展为数百个多样化的"反事实"人-物交互视频；随后用contact-anchored real-to-sim流程重建人与物体运动，将不完美的视频数据重定向为物理上合理的轨迹；利用这些反事实视频的类内多样性训练单一策略。

Result: 利用上述反事实视频的类内差异，训练出可泛化到该类内未见物体的单一策略；在真实机器人上无需任何真实世界微调即完成部署，仅用机载深度观测，人形机器人即可在不同新实例、尺寸和初始位姿下拾取、搬运并放下箱子、桶、储物箱和球等物体。

Conclusion: PRISM证明少量真实视频经扩增和sim-to-real重定向后，足以支撑泛化性loco-manipulation策略学习，并实现真实人形机器人的零微调部署。

Abstract: Teaching humanoids loco-manipulation skills, such as carrying diverse objects, via visual imitation is a promising path toward generalist robots. However, collecting diverse, high-quality interaction videos, such as clips that clearly show a person's full body and unoccluded interactions with objects, poses a practical barrier to scaling this approach. We propose PRISM, a real-to-sim-to-real framework that overcomes this limitation by amplifying a handful of real videos into a large, diverse training set. PRISM first generates hundreds of diverse "counterfactual" human-object interaction videos via video-to-video (V2V) generation from a few exemplar real videos. Our contact-anchored real-to-sim pipeline then reconstructs both human and object motions, retargeting this imperfect video data into physically plausible trajectories. The intra-class variability across these counterfactual videos lets us train a single policy that generalizes to unseen objects within each category. We demonstrate the full pipeline by deploying this policy on a real robot without any real-world fine-tuning. Using only onboard depth observations, our humanoid picks up, carries, and drops objects, including boxes, barrels, bins, and balls, across novel instances, sizes, and initial configurations.

</details>


### [114] [In-context Robot Learning Made Simple: A Democratized Recipe for Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38173)
*Minxing Li,Minghao Han,Weizhi Zhao,Hanwen Wang,Xiangshuo Liu,Shuyao Shang,Jingxiang Zhou,Mingchao Sun,Hongyu Pan,Mu Xu,Yu Liu,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次清晰定义了机器人上下文学习（ICL）的学习目标，解决了视觉示范中"信息混杂"的提示歧义问题，并提出简洁可复现的SimpleICL框架，在仿真和真实环境中均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人ICL虽具潜力，但问题定义不清：一个视觉示范同时传递动作轨迹、物体语义、可供性、空间关系和任务目标，导致机器人究竟该遵循何种信息并不明确，阻碍了该方向的系统研究。

Method: 提出明确的机器人ICL问题定义，界定学习目标并消除提示歧义；在此基础上构建SimpleICL极简框架，包含视觉提示编码器和低成本数据采集协议，无需大规模预训练或专用数据基础设施。

Result: 在仿真与真实环境中均取得强劲性能；实验揭示了机器人ICL的关键特性，包括动作、语义、组合和可供性辨别能力。数据与训练流程将完全开源。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器人ICL提供了清晰的问题定义和可复现的极简框架，无需庞大预训练即可奏效，并揭示其核心能力特性，开源数据与流程以推动该领域系统化、可复现的研究。

Abstract: We study robotic in-context learning (ICL), an emerging paradigm that enables robots to infer and execute tasks from visual demonstrations. Despite its growing promise, the problem itself remains under-defined: a visual demonstration simultaneously conveys action trajectories, object semantics, manipulation affordances, spatial relations, and task goals, making it unclear what information the robot is actually expected to follow. In this work, we first provide a clear problem definition of robot ICL that explicitly defines its learning target and resolves this fundamental prompt ambiguity. Building on this definition, we develop a minimalist and reproducible ICL framework (SimpleICL) with a visual prompt encoder and a low-cost data collection protocol. Without massive pre-training or specialized data infrastructure, our framework achieves strong performance in both simulation and real-world environments. Extensive experiments further reveal several key properties of robot ICL, including action, semantic, composition, and affordance discrimination. We will fully open-source our data and training pipeline to facilitate systematic and reproducible research on robot ICL. The project page can be found at https://simpleicl.github.io/simpleicl.

</details>


### [115] [Skill-Space Shooting for Autonomous Robot Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38178)
*Zihang Rui,Renhao Wang,Haoxu Huang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 skill-space shooting 方法，利用基础模型引导，通过可复用技能探索纠正行为，将成功试验转化为策略改进，实现机器人自主且可跨任务泛化的策略提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在物理世界的机器人需要在遇到新情况和失败时超越初始训练进行自我改进。该改进要能跨任务规模化，就必须有效利用经验，而不是依赖人类对每次纠正的演示。已有的 agentic 系统虽能用基础模型自主组合已学行为来减少人力，但这种方式无法教会任务策略克服自身失败，因此需要将行为转化为策略可学习的纠正。

Method: 作者的核心洞察是：许多纠正其实是熟悉的短行为（即技能），它们跨任务复现，且基础模型能从场景中推理出这些动作。基于此提出 skill-space shooting，使用基础模型引导，通过可复用技能来探索纠正，并将成功试验转化为策略改进。

Result: 真实世界实验显示，该方法能在策略自主运行时实现反复改进，并且技能可以被共享，从而减少在新任务上改进所需的示教。

Conclusion: 通过将可复用技能作为纠正监督的来源，skill-space shooting 实现了任务内和跨任务的规模化、可泛化的策略改进。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the physical world must be able to improve beyond their initial training as they encounter new situations and failures. For this improvement to scale across tasks, it must make effective use of experience without requiring human demonstration of each correction. Recent agentic systems offer a way to reduce this reliance on human effort by using foundation models to autonomously compose learned behaviors to complete tasks. Yet completing tasks this way does not itself teach a task policy to overcome its own failures; that requires turning these behaviors into learnable corrections for the policy. Our insight is that many such corrections are familiar short behaviors, or skills: they recur across tasks and describe actions that foundation models can reason about from a scene. We introduce skill-space shooting, which uses foundation model guidance to explore corrections through these reusable skills and turn successful trials into policy improvement. Real-world experiments show repeated improvement in policies acting autonomously, while skills can also be shared to reduce the teaching needed to improve on new tasks. By making reusable skills a source of corrective supervision, skill-space shooting enables scalable and generalizable policy improvement within and across tasks. Additional results and videos at https://skill-space-shooting.github.io.

</details>
